# Regulatory Watch — Cross-Country Reform Tracker

A single date-stamped registry of property-relevant reforms, transposition deadlines, ENDED programs, and watchlist items. Maintained as the **single source of truth for "what changed when"** — playbooks reference this; this references playbooks.

**Why it exists**: At 126 countries × 41 sections, the highest-blast-radius hallucination is asserting that an ENDED program is still active or quoting a tax rate that was reformed last quarter. This file makes "did anything change?" a single-file lookup before any re-stamp.

**Maintainer**: human + Claude. Each entry must have a primary-source URL, a verified date, and a next-revisit date.

---

## How to use this file

### Before producing any output

`--tax`, `--rental`, `--visa`, `--finance`, `--mains` (where regulated) and `--home-tax` sections must consult this file, and so must every `--journey` template (incl. the foreign-buyer sell-vs-rent extension) that states a tax rate, a letting right or a residency route — for the property's country and, for `--home-tax` and sell-vs-rent, the buyer's home country. If a relevant entry's `revisit_by` date has passed AND the playbook still asserts the pre-reform value, the section's confidence auto-downgrades (see `updater.md` § Auto-downgrade).

### Before re-stamping a section's `**Last verified**`

Check this file for entries touching the section since the prior stamp. If a reform is logged here that the playbook doesn't reflect, the section is **not yet re-verified** — patch the playbook first.

### When adding an entry

Required fields:
- `country` (ISO2)
- `topic` (one of: tax, rental, visa, finance, ownership, esg, cadastre, eurozone, other)
- `effective_date` (YYYY-MM-DD or "pending" + estimate)
- `enacted_date` (when the law/regulation was passed)
- `verified_date` (last time we confirmed status)
- `revisit_by` (next mandatory check)
- `source_url` (primary government / official journal preferred)
- `impact_tier` (1–4, see below)
- `playbook_sections_touched` (list of `--<flag>` flags)

---

## Impact tiers — how to rate a reform

The tier drives revisit cadence and anti-hallucination priority.

| Tier | Meaning | Revisit cadence | Example |
|---|---|---|---|
| **1 — CRITICAL** | Reverses a prior assertion the skill makes (e.g., visa program ENDED, tax rate inverted, ownership opened/closed). Stale claim is a confident lie. | 90 days — full re-research from primary sources, not just a figure check | MT MEIN ECJ ENDED 29 Apr 2025 |
| **2 — MAJOR** | Material number change (>20% rate move), new mandatory diagnostic, EU directive transposition affecting many countries. | 90 days | RO VAT 19 → 21% Aug 2025; EPBD 2024/1275 |
| **3 — MEANINGFUL** | Threshold/credit/exemption tweak; parliamentary stage advance; rumored-to-draft transition. | 180 days | CY 2026 Comprehensive Tax Reform (CGT exemptions raised) |
| **4 — INCREMENTAL** | Indexation adjustment, fee tweak, technical correction, watchlist signal. | 365 days | LU Bëllegen Akt indexation; LV cadastral value rebase rumored |

**Rule**: If a user asks "what visa programs are open in <country>", consult this table first; only entries NOT here are candidates for "open" status, and even those need a fresh `verified_date` ≤180 days.

- `2026-02-21 (acquisitions to 2028-12-31) | tax | Statut du bailleur privé ("relance logement", also called dispositif Jeanbrun) — NEW long-let investor deduction created by LF 2026 art. 47 (loi n° 2026-103 du 19 février 2026, JORF n°0043 du 20 Feb 2026), new CGI art. 31 I 1° i (new-build) and j (bought with works). A landlord taxed on revenus fonciers under the régime réel deducts a yearly amortisation on a base of 80% of the net purchase price (land deemed 20% and not amortisable; plus qualifying works under j): 3.5% a year for intermediate rents, +1 point social, +2 points very social, on a dwelling in a collective residential building bought new or VEFA, or with a building-permit application, between 21 Feb 2026 and 31 Dec 2028; 3%, +0.5 or +1 point, on a dwelling bought in that window that undergoes works producing a new building or improvement works of at least 30% of the price, and meets the heavy-rehabilitation criteria (CGI art. 150 U II 7° b, notably energy class A or B, per ANIL). Deductions capped at 8,000 EUR per foyer fiscal per year across both, 10,000 / 12,000 EUR where at least half the gross rents come from social / very social lets. Conditions: unfurnished main-residence lease, effective within 12 months of completion and kept at least 9 years, tenant outside the household and family to the second degree, rent and tenant-income ceilings (Pinel ceilings for intermediate, Loc'Avantages for social / very social, per ANIL); irrevocable option on the tax return. Breach adds the deducted amortisation back to revenus fonciers (quotient method); on a sale it reduces the acquisition price for capital-gains tax (CGI art. 150 VB III, as for LMNP). Not combinable on the same dwelling with Girardin social (CGI art. 199 undecies C); under j also not with the overseas housing reductions (art. 199 undecies A), Malraux (art. 199 tervicies) or Pinel / Duflot / Denormandie (art. 199 novovicies); excluded for dismembered ownership; open through a company not subject to IS. Service-Public A18817 (24 Feb 2026) adds that charges above rental income create a déficit foncier deductible from other income up to 10,700 EUR. - `pending (PLF 2027 bill) | finance | PTZ parentalité — BILL, not adopted as of 2026-10-28: projet de loi de finances pour 2027 (AN n° 3210, filed 1 Oct 2026), art. 7 I.A would add a d) to CCH art. - `2025-04-01 (to 2028-03-31) | tax | DMTO temporary rate-cap rise — LF 2025 (loi n° 2025-127 du 14 février 2025) art. 116 II A lets a département raise its CGI art. 1594 D rate "au-delà de 4,50 %, sans que ce taux excède 5 %" for acts and agreements concluded from 1 Apr 2025 to 31 Mar 2028; the deliberations run to 31 Mar 2028 unless an earlier end is set (BOFiP). At 5.00% the total DMTO is about 6.32%, against about 5.81% at 4.50% (computed in the FR playbook, with the 1.20% communal tax and the frais d'assiette). Primo-accédants buying their main residence are exempt from the rise (art. 116 II B: first property, intended as main residence; BOFiP applies a 2-year no-ownership test). Next step: deliberations notified 16 Apr to 30 Nov 2026 apply from 1 Jan 2027, those notified 1 Dec 2026 to 15 Apr 2027 from the first day of the second month after notification (BOFiP; the later window added by LF 2026 art. 121 per ANIL). Earlier movers keep their amount for the rest of the option (max 15 years): transfers up to and including 10 Aug 2024 €100,000; transfers after 10 Aug 2024 and up to 31 Dec 2025 €200,000 (DL 113/2024 art. 2); family members in both earlier groups €25,000. From 1 Jan 2027 the regime moves, still at €300,000, to art. 246 of the new Testo unico delle imposte sui redditi (D.Lgs. 19 Jun 2026 n. 117, GU n. 152 of 3 Jul 2026, SO 26; applies from 1 Jan 2027 under art. 377). DL 27 Mar 2026 n. 38 art. 2 (conv. L. 22 May 2026 n. 88) bars combining it with the impatriati regime (art. 5 D.Lgs. 209/2023) for transfers of tax residence from tax period 2027. The DDL di bilancio 2027 approved by CdM n. 195 on 28 Oct 2026 announces no change to the regime — none in the Presidente del Consiglio's press-conference statement on governo.it or in the press reports read; DDL text not published as of 2026-10-28 (see the Legge di Bilancio 2027 stage entry) | Agenzia delle Entrate (updated 16 Jan 2026) — https://www.agenziaentrate.gov.it/portale/schede/agevolazioni/opzione-per-i-neo-residenti/infogen-opzionale-neo-residenti + Camera dei deputati dossier https://documenti.camera.it/leg19/dossier/pdf/ID0014evol1.pdf + governo.it CdM n. 195 https://www.governo.it/it/articolo/conferenza-stampa-del-consiglio-dei-ministri-195-lintervento-del-presidente-meloni/32969 | 2026-10-28 | 2026-11-04 | 2 | --tax --home-tax --digital-nomad (IT)`
- `2027-01-01 | tax | Renovation deductions step down for spese 2027 under current law — L. 199/2025 (Legge di Bilancio 2026) art. 1 c. 22, in force, amending DL 63/2013 art. 14 c. 3-quinquies (Ecobonus) and art. 16 c. 1 and c. 1-septies.1 (Bonus Ristrutturazioni / Sismabonus). Bonus Ristrutturazioni (art. 16-bis TUIR): spese 2025-2026 50% when the work is on the taxpayer's abitazione principale and the taxpayer holds ownership (incl. nuda proprietà) or a real right of enjoyment (usufrutto, uso, abitazione), 36% in all other cases incl. second or holiday homes and tenants; spese 2027 36% / 30%; cap €96,000 per unit both years, 10 annual instalments; spese 2028-2033 30% on €48,000 for all homes; from 2034 36% on €48,000 (AdE guida Feb 2026). Ecobonus and Sismabonus carry the same 50/36 (2025-2026) and 36/30 (2027) rates (Camera dei deputati dossier); since 2025 the Ecobonus no longer covers stand-alone fossil-fuel boilers. Bonus Mobili ed elettrodomestici: 50% on up to €5,000 per unit for purchases made by 31 Dec 2026 only, linked to works begun from 1 Jan of the previous year — DL 63/2013 art. 16 c. 2 as in force has no 2027 year. A foreign buyer's second home therefore gets 36% in 2026 and 30% in 2027 under current law, not 50%. CdM n. 195 approved the DDL di bilancio 2027 on 28 Oct 2026; its official communiqué says only 'Per il 2027 sono confermate le detrazioni per le ristrutturazioni edilizie' (no rates, no Bonus Mobili). Sismabonus acquisti) are covered awaits the DDL text (Edilportale 28 Oct 2026), not published as of 2026-10-28. - `2025-09-22 | rental | Madrid STR freeze SUPERSEDED by a permanent zoning regime — do not describe Madrid as frozen. MADRID: the April 2024 licence freeze was an express art. 70 LSCM cautionary measure ("Suspensión temporal de nuevas licencias ... es una medida cautelar adoptada antes de que se defina la futura ordenación de las VUT", Ayuntamiento de Madrid), and art. 70.4 LSCM caps such a suspension at "no podrá exceder de un año", extendable one further year — it cannot still be running in 2026. It was replaced by the Modificación del PGOU "para la mejora del uso residencial" (expediente 135-2024-00206, Plan RESIDE), definitively approved by Acuerdo del Consejo de Gobierno de la Comunidad de Madrid of 27 Aug 2025 (BOCM 211 of 4 Sep 2025); normative text in BOCM 226 of 22 Sep 2025, which the Ayuntamiento transparency portal labels normativa aplicable. New art. 7.6.3 bis binds ONLY the APE 00.01 Centro Histórico (ámbito per art. 4.3.17). Inside it: hospedaje in part of an EXISTING RESIDENTIAL building is barred on every floor (para. 10.b.i "No se admite su implantación en ninguna de las plantas del edificio") — dispersed VUT in central residential buildings is permanently barred, not paused; in existing NON-residential buildings hospedaje stays admissible as a complementary use (any situation at levels A and B, upper floors only at C and D, para. 10.a). Edificio exclusivo then varies by the art. 8.1.29 use-compatibility level — levels A and B (para. 8): on parcels with an existing non-residential building plain "uso alternativo" (plan especial de protección if catalogued level 1 or 2); on unbuilt parcels or existing residential buildings VUT is "uso alternativo" only in buildings catalogued protection level 3, the activity licence lapses after "quince (15) años" back to vivienda colectiva and that condition "se harán constar en el Registro de la Propiedad", autorizable at protection levels 1 or 2 via plan especial, and "Uso prohibido en parcelas no edificadas y en edificios no catalogados"; levels C and D (para. 9): plain "uso alternativo", plan especial only if catalogued level 1 or 2, with NO 15-year lapse and NO Registro annotation; level E (para. 11) and Colonias Históricas (para. 6.b): hospedaje not admitted. Para. 4: art. 7.6.3 bis does not apply to parcels holding a validated Estudio de Repercusión por Implantación de Usos or a definitively approved Plan Especial for hospedaje not yet implemented, while those instruments stay in force; para. 7: the conditions refer to parcels whose uso cualificado is residential. Áreas de Planeamiento (API, APE, APR) interior to the APE take the A and B conditions (para. 6.a). The level map sits in art. 8.1.29, which BOCM 226 does NOT republish — establish the parcel's level before relying on any of the above. Citywide art. 7.6.3.4: locales in residential buildings "contarán siempre con acceso independiente". Disposición derogatoria única folds the 2019 Plan Especial de Hospedaje (PE 00/313, approved 27 Mar 2019, BOCM 23-04-2019) into art. 7.6.3 bis. Litigation: no primary court source checked — press only (news-only, lower confidence): TSJM admitted a PSOE contentious appeal (reported Oct 2025) and, by auto dated 4 Mar 2026, refused the interim suspension sought by a VUT operator (El País, 9 Mar 2026); no merits ruling located at 2026-10-21 — treat annulment risk as unassessed. BARCELONA: unchanged — PEUAT "estableix el creixement zero dels HUT a tota la ciutat", but a cessation in a saturated zone "es permetrà una nova alta o la redistribució a la zona de creixement contingut", so new HUTs are not absolutely impossible citywide; per-zone detail lives in the BCN November 2028 HUT-expiry entry (no calendar day is sourced) (2026 data, Ajuntament de Barcelona PEUAT page consulted 2026-10-21) | BOCM num. 226 de 22 Sep 2025 anuncio 47 https://www.bocm.es/boletin/CM_Orden_BOCM/2025/09/22/BOCM-20250922-47.PDF + Ayuntamiento de Madrid Portal de Transparencia expediente 135-2024-00206 + art. 70 LSCM consolidado BOE-A-2001-18984 https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2001-18984 + Ajuntament de Barcelona PEUAT https://ajuntament.barcelona.cat/urbanisme-accio-climatica-mobilitat-pla-barris-serveis-urbans/ca/pla-especial-urbanistic-dallotjaments-turistics + El País 9 Mar 2026 (TSJM auto on interim measures, news-only) | 2026-10-21 | 2027-01-19 | 1 | --rental (ES)`
- `2024-06-21 | rental | BCN universal HUT expiry — Decret llei 3/2023 de 7 de novembre (DOGC 9036 de 8 Nov 2023; BOE núm. 5 of 5 Jan 2024, BOE-A-2024-281, records "Entrada en vigor: 09/11/2023"; validated by Parlament Resolució 897/XIV, DOGC 9066 of 22 Dec 2023; BOE consolidated text shows no amendment at 2026-10-21) DT2 apartat 1 gives already-habilitated HUTs in the annex municipalities (housing-access problems or high HUT concentration per DT1; Barcelona is annex no. 24 — CORRECTION: not "tensioned municipalities", which is the separate Ley 12/2023 zone concept) five years to hold a compliant llicència urbanística "para poder ejercer esta actividad en el plazo de cinco años desde la entrada en vigor de este decreto-ley, o cesar su actividad" — terminal month November 2028. Ajuntament announced 21 Jun 2024 (Pla Viure) that it will grant none: Consorci de l'Habitatge de Barcelona, 21 Jun 2024 — "al cap de cinc anys des de la seva entrada en vigor, tots els HUT de la ciutat desapareixeran i quedaran prohibits. El novembre del 2028 Barcelona ja no tindrà cap pis turístic". NO Ajuntament or Generalitat source states a calendar day; the previously carried "24 Nov 2028" is WITHDRAWN as unsourced; secondary sites variously give 31 Oct, 7 Nov, 9 Nov or 24 Nov 2028 — none is primary. DT2 apartat 2 (cite as DT2.2, NOT DT1.2 — DT1 defines qualifying municipalities) allows ONE owner-requested extension of up to five further years, to 2033, where the holder proves the transitional regime does not compensate the loss of the enabling title — "pot sol·licitar una única ampliació del règim transitori de fins a cinc anys més", to be requested at the ajuntament after four years from entry into force and before the apartat 1 transitional period ends (computed: window opens 9 Nov 2027). Constitutional challenge FAILED — STC 64/2025 de 13 de marzo (BOE-A-2025-7430), recurso 798-2024 against arts. 1-3, DT1 apartat 1 a), DT2, DF1 and the annex: fallo "ha decidido desestimar el presente recurso de inconstitucionalidad" (CORRECTION: the previously quoted "desestima en su totalidad" wording comes from the voto particular, not the fallo). PEUAT revision work announced for 2025 in the same Mesura de Govern; no revised PEUAT located as approved at 2026-10-21 — re-verify zone rules before relying on them. - `2026-04-01 | rental | Catalonia IEET tourist tax — tariff schedule REPLACED by Llei 2/2026 de 6 de març (DOGC 9621 de 10 Mar 2026), in force 1 Apr 2026; the pre-2026 figures are superseded. Barcelona city totals per person per night 1 Apr 2026-31 Mar 2027 (general tariff plus €5.00 Ajuntament recàrrec): 5★/gran luxe €12.00, 4★ €8.40, habitatge d'ús turístic €9.50, albergs de joventut €6.00, other establishments €7.00, cruise call over 12h €9.00 / 12h or less €11.00; the Ajuntament recàrrec steps up to €6.00 for 1 Apr 2027-31 Mar 2028 and €7.00 for 1 Apr 2028-31 Mar 2029. Barcelona albergs, news-only (lower confidence): the September 2026 Plenari voted to hold the alberg recàrrec at €4.00 (ACN / Metròpoli Abierta, Sep 2026; reported BOPB publication 29 Sep 2026, CVE 202610144929, not primary-verified), which would give a €5.00 alberg total from 1 Oct 2026, but the ATC table read 2026-10-23 still shows a €5.00 recàrrec (€6.00 total) — verify with the ATC before quoting either. Rest of Catalonia general tariff 1 Apr 2026-31 Mar 2027: 5★ €4.50, 4★ €1.80, HUT €1.75, albergs €0.80, other €0.90, cruise over 12h €3.00 / 12h or less €4.50; scheduled step-up from 1 Apr 2027 to 5★ €6.00, 4★ €2.40, HUT €2.50, albergs €1.00, other €1.20, cruise €4.00 / €6.00. Barcelona recàrrec statutory ceiling €8/night (art. 34 bis). Municipal recàrrec outside Barcelona (art. 34 ter, added by Llei 2/2026): exigible only for stays from 1 Oct 2026, at most €4 per category and never above the tariff for that establishment type, effective from the first day of the semestral liquidation period (1 Apr-30 Sep, 1 Oct-31 Mar) after the ordinance is published in the BOP, provided the ATC conveni has been signed and published in the DOGC. IN FORCE since 1 Oct 2026 in seven municipalities (ATC convenis in DOGC 9758 of 25 Sep, 9760 of 29 Sep and 9761 of 30 Sep 2026): Castelldefels, Esplugues de Llobregat, L'Hospitalet de Llobregat, El Prat de Llobregat, Sabadell, Sant Cugat del Vallès, Sant Just Desvern. Recàrrec 1 Oct 2026-31 Mar 2027 in all except Esplugues: 5★ €4.00, 4★ €1.80, HUT €1.75, albergs €0.80, other €0.90; from 1 Apr 2027 in L'Hospitalet, Sabadell, Sant Cugat, El Prat and Castelldefels: 5★ €4.00, 4★ €2.40, HUT €2.50 (Castelldefels €2.40), albergs €1.00, other €1.20; Sant Just Desvern publishes a single from-1-Oct-2026 column. El Prat and Castelldefels also charge cruise calls: over 12h €3.00 (€4.00 from 1 Apr 2027), 12h or less €4.00. Esplugues (Ordenança fiscal núm. 26, approved by the Ple 29 Jul 2026) rates only three rows, before / from 1 Apr 2027: 4★ €1.80 / €2.40, HUT €0.90 / €1.20, other €1.75 / €2.50, with no 5★, alberg or cruise recàrrec; its "other" row exceeds the €0.90 / €1.20 tariff that art. 34 ter.1.a caps it at, so the HUT and other rows look swapped — verify with the Ajuntament d'Esplugues / ATC before relying on either. Computed HUT totals per person per night (tariff + recàrrec): L'Hospitalet, Sabadell, Sant Cugat, El Prat €1.75 + €1.75 = €3.50, then €2.50 + €2.50 = €5.00 from 1 Apr 2027; Castelldefels €3.50, then €4.90; Sant Just €3.50, then €4.25 (€2.50 + €1.75) unless a 2027 column is published; Esplugues as published €2.65, then €3.70. Every other municipality outside Barcelona charges the tariff only; the ATC list is updated as convenis enter into force, and computed from the liquidation-period rule no further municipality can start before 1 Apr 2027 — hard recheck before that date. Cap remains 7 stay-units per person; persons aged 16 or under exempt (2026 data, source ATC Quota tributària, tarifes i recàrrecs municipals + ATC IEET page + ATC news 30 Sep 2026, read 2026-10-23) | Generalitat de Catalunya ATC https://atc.gencat.cat/ca/tributs/ieet/quota-tributaria + https://atc.gencat.cat/ca/tributs/ieet/ + https://atc.gencat.cat/ca/agencia/noticies/detall-noticia/20260925-ieet-recarrec-municipal + Llei 2/2026 BOE-A-2026-6642 https://www.boe.es/diario_boe/txt.php?id=BOE-A-2026-6642 + Esplugues Ordenança fiscal núm. 26 https://intranet.esplugues.cat/WebAplic/Hisenda/ordfiscals2026.nsf/v/A67DA326B2875A5EC1258E740052B207/$file/CAOF26.PDF | 2026-10-23 | 2027-01-21 | 2 | --rental`
- `2019-03-27 (norms absorbed into art. 7.6.3 bis by BOCM 22 Sep 2025) | rental | Madrid PEH — legacy entry CORRECTED and NARROWED; two limbs WITHDRAWN as unsourced. DATE and SCOPE were wrong: the Plan Especial de Hospedaje is a 2019 instrument covering ELEVEN districts, not a 2018 Distrito Centro plan. Disposición derogatoria única of BOCM 226 of 22 Sep 2025 anuncio 47 names it verbatim "Plan Especial de Regulación del Uso de Servicios Terciarios en la clase de Hospedaje. Distritos de Centro, Arganzuela, Retiro, Salamanca, Chamartín, Tetuán, Chamberí, Moncloa-Aravaca, Latina, Carabanchel y Usera, aprobado definitivamente el 27 de marzo de 2019 y publicado en el BOCM el 23-04-2019 (PE 00/313)" — that eleven-district list is the REPEALED plan's scope, NOT a current perimeter. NOT A LIVE INSTRUMENT: its norms "quedan incorporadas, con las adaptaciones necesarias, en el texto del artículo 7.6.3 bis de las Normas Urbanísticas del PG97" and "Quedan sin efecto los documentos de Diagnóstico y Memoria del citado Plan Especial" — never cite the PEH as current. The operative regime is the 2025-09-22 Madrid entry above and is deliberately NOT restated here; note only that art. 7.6.3 bis.2 fixes its own ámbito as "el del Área de Planeamiento Específico del Centro Histórico (APE 00.01) definido en el artículo 4.3.17" and art. 4.3.17 is not reproduced in this anuncio, so the perimeter cannot be resolved from it — the string Distrito Centro does not appear in the anuncio at all. WITHDRAWN as unsourced: ">90 nights/yr classed as commercial use". Full-text searches of both instruments run 2026-08-07 find no nights-per-year or days-per-year threshold: in the anuncio, días, noches, al año and pernocta are absent and the single occurrence of 90 is "unidades menores de 90 m² útiles"; in Decreto 79/2014 as amended by DECRETO 27/2026 de 25 de marzo (BOCM 80 of 6 Abr 2026, "entrará en vigor a los veinte días siguientes al de su publicación", so in force since late April 2026) al año, anuales and noventa are absent from both texts. The only period rule in the 2014 original was a per-stay floor in old art. 17.3 ("no podrán contratarse por un período inferior a cinco días"), which is not a per-year cap and which the 2026 decree does not carry forward when it rewrites art. 17 wholesale ("Nueve. El artículo 17 queda redactado del siguiente modo"). The 2026 decree instead empowers municipalities to limit "el número máximo de viviendas de uso turístico por edificio, al ámbito, zona, sector o períodos", so any day-count could only sit in a municipal limit adopted under that delegation — data not publicly available in the sources checked; verify at Ayuntamiento de Madrid and the Comunidad de Madrid dirección general competente en materia de Turismo. ALSO WITHDRAWN: "portal/lift separation from residents" — portal does not occur in the anuncio and the single ascensor occurrence is an unrelated rehabilitation rule ("obras de acondicionamiento y/o de reestructuración puntual para implantación de ascensores"). What IS sourced is weaker and citywide, not Distrito-Centro-specific: art. 7.6.3.4 "Los locales integrados en edificios de uso residencial, con independencia de su modalidad y situación, contarán siempre con acceso independiente". The stronger wording "acceso independiente desde el exterior, sin utilizar elementos comunes de circulación del edificio" is art. 7.1.4 apartado 4 and is expressly displaced for this use class — "Las condiciones de acceso independiente de los locales de la clase de uso terciario hospedaje serán las establecidas en el artículo 7.6.3.4 de estas normas" — so do not attribute a common-stair or lift exclusion to hospedaje. TIER LOWERED 2 to 3: with both limbs withdrawn this line no longer records a reform and no longer reverses a live regime assertion (the supersession is carried by the 2025-09-22 Tier-1 entry); what remains is a date and scope correction plus two withdrawals. ACTION: shared/rental-yield-delta.md line 90 repeats the withdrawn wording and line 51 lists MAD Distrito Centro PEH in the zone-freeze list — patch both | BOCM num. 226 de 22 Sep 2025 anuncio 47 https://www.bocm.es/boletin/CM_Orden_BOCM/2025/09/22/BOCM-20250922-47.PDF + DECRETO 27/2026 de 25 de marzo BOCM num. 80 de 6 Abr 2026 https://www.bocm.es/boletin/CM_Orden_BOCM/2026/04/06/BOCM-20260406-1.PDF + Decreto 79/2014 de 10 de julio BOCM num. 180 de 31 Jul 2014 https://www.bocm.es/boletin/CM_Orden_BOCM/2014/07/31/BOCM-20140731-1.PDF | 2026-08-07 | 2027-02-03 | 3 | --rental (ES)`
- `2027-01-01 (pending convalidation) | tax | Imputed income on non-let urban property (LIRPF art. 85.1) becomes a PROGRESSIVE SCALE — Real Decreto-ley 29/2026, de 6 de octubre (BOE núm. 249, 7 Oct 2026, BOE-A-2026-20823), in force since 8 Oct 2026, convalidation pending (see the 2026-10-08 entry). With effect from 1 Jan 2027 the scale applies to the SUM of the cadastral values of urban property that is neither the habitual residence, nor let or otherwise generating capital income, nor used in a business (also rural property with non-essential buildings; undeveloped land excluded; each value pro-rated by days): up to EUR 100,000 1.1 %; EUR 100,000-500,000 EUR 1,100 + 1.5 % of the excess; EUR 500,000-1,000,000 EUR 7,100 + 2 % of the excess; above EUR 1,000,000 EUR 17,100 + 3 % of the excess. A property with no cadastral value, or one not yet notified to the owner, enters the scale at 50 % of the greater of the value verified by the Administration for other taxes or the acquisition price; until then it is taxed at a flat 1.1 % on that same base (not the Wealth Tax value). Through tax year 2026 the existing rule applies: 2 %, or 1.1 % where revised cadastral values took effect in the tax year or the 10 prior years; DA 55.ª as amended by the decree extends to 2026 (after 2023-2025) the 1.1 % rate wherever revised values took effect from 1 Jan 2012. Non-residents: the decree does not amend the TRLIRNR, but TRLIRNR art. 24.5 sets their imputed income by the IRPF rule and a notary FAQ (secondary) states the 2027 scale reaches them; the decree does not address how a summed scale fits per-property Modelo 210 filing. The predecessor RDL 26/2026, which made the same art. 85 change, was derogated by the Congreso on 2 Oct 2026 (BOE-A-2026-20526); if RDL 29/2026 is not convalidated the 2027 scale never applies | Real Decreto-ley 29/2026 BOE-A-2026-20823 https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2026-20823 + Resolución Congreso BOE-A-2026-20526 https://www.boe.es/diario_boe/txt.php?id=BOE-A-2026-20526 + Notarías Barcelona FAQ (secondary) https://notariasbarcelona.es/real-decreto-ley-29-2026-novedades-fiscales | 2026-10-28 | 2026-11-14 | 2 | --tax (ES)`

- `2026-10-08 (in force, pending convalidation) | rental | Real Decreto-ley 29/2026, de 6 de octubre (BOE núm. 249, 7 Oct 2026, BOE-A-2026-20823) — housing package IN FORCE since 8 Oct 2026 but PROVISIONAL: the Diputación Permanente must convalidate or derogate it as a whole because the Cortes are dissolved for the 29 Nov 2026 general election (art. 86.2 with art. 78.2 CE); still pending on 2026-10-28 (Congreso expte. 130/000060, "Situación actual: Diputación Permanente Convalidación" since 13 Oct 2026; not on the 16 Oct 2026 Diputación Permanente agenda, which took only RDL 24/2026 and 25/2026; the Congreso agenda for 9-15 Nov 2026 showed no session convened on 2026-10-28); EFE (27 Oct 2026, secondary) reports 12 Nov 2026 as the most likely vote date. The 29 Sep 2026 predecessors were derogated by the Congreso on 2 Oct 2026 (RDL 26/2026 BOE-A-2026-20526; RDL 27/2026 BOE-A-2026-20527). Measures in force now: (1) LAU — a temporada lease needs a real, provable cause stated in the contract, burden of proof on the landlord, and must exceed 31 days and generally not exceed 12 months (new art. 9 bis); tourist stays excluded from the LAU capped at 31 days; the combined rent of simultaneous room lets cannot exceed the whole-unit rent; (2) DF sexta — annual rent updates falling due by 31 Dec 2027: no increase where rent is above the reference-index maximum, otherwise as newly agreed or capped at 2 % absent a new agreement; (3) DF quinta — on the tenant's request, an extraordinary extension in annual periods of up to 2 years for leases in force on 8 Oct 2026 whose mandatory, tacit or reconducción period ends before 31 Dec 2028, where the tenant has paid monthly for the previous 8 months; the landlord may refuse only for own or family need, or where other terms or a new contract are agreed; (4) evictions of vulnerable occupants without a housing alternative suspended until 31 Dec 2030; (5) until 31 Dec 2030 legal-person grandes tenedores, their group entities and entities buying property or defaulted mortgage portfolios clearly below appraisal value may not acquire a dwelling free of charge or for less than 70 % of market value set by an independent appraisal firm registered with the Banco de España (exceptions: affordable or social habitual-home use to 2030, social-care and vulnerable-group housing, adherents to a Código de Buenas Prácticas once approved); the Ley 12/2023 art. 3.k gran tenedor threshold is unchanged (more than 10 urban residential properties or more than 1,500 m², CCAA may lower it to 5 or more in tensioned zones), now proved by Registro de la Propiedad certificate with each unit of an undivided building counted separately; (6) IBI — new TRLRHL art. 72.4 ter: in a declared zona de mercado residencial tensionado the ayuntamiento may, by fiscal ordinance, surcharge the cuota líquida of residential property used as tourist lodging by up to 50 %, up to 100 % for owners of 2 or more such units and up to 150 % for 4 or more, accruing 31 Dec and applying to tax periods starting after 8 Oct 2026 (2027 IBI at the earliest); art. 72.4 bis on empty homes rewritten; (7) TU CASA — 0 % ICO loans for a first mortgaged habitual home for the lower of 20 % of value or EUR 50,000 (EUR 10,000M extraordinary credit), with a permanent CPI-linked maximum resale price; beneficiaries and limits still to be set by Acuerdo de Consejo de Ministros; (8) 25 % special levy on SOCIMI undistributed profits from residential letting, reduced by 50 % or 100 % where affordable homes exceed 80 % of the rental stock (60 % in 2026, 70 % in 2027); (9) state IRPF deduction of 10 % of habitual-home rent for a base imponible under EUR 33,007.20. Deferred limbs are separate entries: 2026-12-01 (IVA on lets of 30 nights or fewer, 4 % IVA on protected housing, IIVTNU coefficients) and 2027-01-01 (art. 85 LIRPF imputed-income scale); the LAU art. 10 regime is RDL 28/2026 (2026-11-15 entry) | Real Decreto-ley 29/2026 BOE-A-2026-20823 https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2026-20823 + BOE núm. 249 https://www.boe.es/boe/dias/2026/10/07 + La Moncloa 8 Oct 2026 https://www.lamoncloa.gob.es/serviciosdeprensa/notasprensa/vivienda-agenda-urbana/paginas/2026/decreto-vivienda-medidas.aspx + Resolución Congreso BOE-A-2026-20526 https://www.boe.es/diario_boe/txt.php?id=BOE-A-2026-20526 + Congreso expte. 130/000060 https://www.congreso.es/es/proyectos-de-ley?p_p_id=iniciativas&p_p_lifecycle=0&p_p_state=normal&p_p_mode=view&_iniciativas_mode=mostrarDetalle&_iniciativas_legislatura=XV&_iniciativas_id=130/000060 + Diputación Permanente sesión 8 orden del día https://www.congreso.es/backoffice_doc/atp/orden_dia/DP_008_102026.pdf + EFE via Infobae 27 Oct 2026 (secondary) https://www.infobae.com/espana/agencias/2026/10/27/el-congreso-preve-debatir-los-decretos-de-vivienda-el-dia-despues-de-la-huelga-general/ + Congreso agenda 9-15 Nov 2026 (read 2026-10-28) https://www.congreso.es/es/agenda?p_p_id=agenda&p_p_lifecycle=0&p_p_state=normal&p_p_mode=view&_agenda_mvcPath=agendaSemanal&_agenda_tipoagenda=1&_agenda_dia=12&_agenda_mes=11&_agenda_anio=2026 | 2026-10-28 | 2026-11-14 | 2 | --rental --tax --finance (ES)`

Licensed accommodation: L. 43/2024 (published 22 May 2024, in force 23 May 2024) required licensed accommodation inside þéttbýli to be in approved atvinnuhúsnæði (commercial premises); L. 25/2026 art. 1a limits that rule to ótímabundin (open-ended) licensed accommodation, and art. 2 makes a rekstrarleyfi for accommodation in íbúðarhúsnæði (residential housing) inside þéttbýli time-limited to five years at a time; art. 5 (new ákvæði til bráðabirgða) keeps such licences valid on 1 Apr 2026 until 1 Jan 2032, before which a new licence must be sought. IX art. 71 only replaces the heilbrigðisnefnd references in Act 85/2007 with Umhverfis- og orkustofnun and Stofnun atvinnuveganna — no change to the heimagisting caps or scope. - `2028-03-01 (first liability date; Bill — NOT enacted) | tax | Derelict Property Tax (DPT): announced Budget 2026, plans approved by Government Jun 2026, now in Finance (No. 2) Bill 2026 (Bill 98 of 2026) s.76, which inserts a new Part 22C into TCA 1997; initiated 19 Oct 2026, Dáil Second Stage debate 20 Oct 2026, NOT enacted as of 2026-10-23, and after enactment s.76 also needs a commencement order from the Minister for Finance (s.76(24)). As initiated: a flat annual 7 percent of the market value of property on a local-authority final register plus its appurtenant land, charged on the 1 March liability date; valuation date is the first liability date, then every 3 years; self-assessed, pay and file with Revenue by 23 June. In scope: derelict housing and commercial property in a specified built-up area that is not in use as, or suitable for use as, a dwelling, business premises or certain infrastructure (national and historic monuments, certain property under construction and defective-concrete-block property excluded). Phasing: 2027 preliminary registers cover built-up areas of 4,000+ people, 2028 and later registers 2,000+. Timeline: local authorities identify properties from Jan 2027; first preliminary registers by 1 Sep 2027; first final registers by 28 Feb 2028; first liability date 1 Mar 2028; first return and payment by 23 Jun 2028. Paid LPT and VHT are credited against DPT, capped at the DPT due, with no refund or carry-forward. Unpaid tax and interest become a charge on the property; surcharge up to 30 percent for significant undervaluation; deferral after a recent change of ownership, during remediation and during appeals. Government policy is for DPT to replace the local-authority Derelict Sites Levy (7 percent of market value), but the Bill as initiated does not repeal that levy | Finance (No. 2) Bill 2026 as initiated + Explanatory Memorandum + Oireachtas bill record + DoF Finance (No. 2) Bill 2026 press release — https://data.oireachtas.ie/ie/oireachtas/bill/2026/98/eng/initiated/b9826d.pdf / https://data.oireachtas.ie/ie/oireachtas/bill/2026/98/eng/memo/b9826d-memo.pdf / https://api.oireachtas.ie/v1/legislation?bill_year=2026&bill_no=98 / https://www.gov.ie/en/department-of-finance/press-releases/department-of-finance-publishes-finance-bill-no2-2026 | 2026-10-23 | 2026-12-31 | 3 | --tax (IE)`

- `2023-05-01 | tax | Land, Deeds & Probate Registries Fees & Duties Order 2023 (SD 2023/0077; made by the Treasury 6 Mar 2023 under s.81 Interpretation Act 2015; in operation 1 May 2023) — tiered duty per £1,000 of value (consideration, or value of the land if higher, rounded up to the nearest £1,000; minimum duty £250 in every tier): Owner-Occupier 0% to £230,000 / 1% £230,000-£500,000 / 2% £500,000-£1,000,000 (needs a dwelling valued under £1,000,000 declared the sole residential property worldwide and occupied by the buyer and household, plus residence); Non-Owner Occupier (resident) 2% to £3,000,000 / 2.5% above; Non-Resident 4% to £3,000,000 / 4.5% above. Residence is the Order's own test, not income-tax residence: present on the Island at least 183 days in the 364-day window ending on the transaction date, or a declaration before the transaction of intent to become resident within 183 days (arts 4-5); a single resident co-buyer moves a joint purchase from the non-resident to the non-owner-occupier rate, and wholly or partly commercial property pays the non-owner-occupier rate (arts 9-10); family-member co-buyers get the owner-occupier rate on the qualifying share, capped at 75% of value (art 10). Still the Order in force per the gov.im Land Registry page (2023 Order WEF 01 May 2023; online fee calculator from 1 May 2023); no amending or replacement order found. Editorial: the main buyer-side transaction cost on the Island (no stamp duty) | gov.im Central Registry PDF of the Order `https://www.gov.im/media/1379454/land-deeds-and-probate-registries-fees-and-duties-order-2023-200423.pdf` + gov.im Land Registry page `https://www.gov.im/categories/home-and-neighbourhood/deeds-and-probate-registry/land-registry` | 2026-10-21 | 2027-01-19 | 2 | --tax --notary (IM)`

- `2026-07-15 | visa | EU-UK Gibraltar Treaty signed at Brussels 14 Jul 2026 and PROVISIONALLY APPLIED since 15 Jul 2026 (Art. 336(2): conditional on the implementation plan and administrative arrangements being in effect, published 14 Jul 2026); NOT yet in force or ratified — Art. 336(1): entry into force on the first day of the month after both Parties notify completion of internal procedures; EU side: Commission submitted it to the European Parliament for consent 14 Jul 2026, procedure 2026/0058(NLE) joint AFET/INTA, awaiting committee decision, indicative plenary 14 Dec 2026 (OEIL), then a Council conclusion decision; UK CRAG: laid 14 Jul 2026, first 21-sitting-day objection period was due to end 22 Oct 2026 (Commons Library CBP-10980; outcome not confirmed at a primary source on 2026-10-29). Art. 28 removes all physical barriers to the circulation of persons; Art. 29 puts border checks at Gibraltar port and airport (entry: Gibraltar authorities then Spain; exit: Spain then Gibraltar), and there are no immigration controls at the Gibraltar-Spain land border (HMGoG Technical Notice 778/2026). Art. 42: persons resident in Gibraltar (legal right to reside, any nationality; EU/EEA/Swiss citizens fall outside the definition) get no entry/exit stamps and are exempt from EES and ETIAS — in practice some residents were wrongly registered in EES after 15 Jul 2026; the Commission has issued further instructions to Schengen authorities and residents may request erasure from the Schengen State of their last recorded crossing (HMGoG Technical Notices 696/2026 of 23 Sep 2026 and 778/2026 of 13 Oct 2026). Residence: the treaty gives no right of establishment and residence stays under Gibraltar's own immigration laws (HMGoG PR 148/2026); Art. 45 proves the right to reside by a Gibraltar identity card or residence permit, a residence permit needs a genuine connection (actual and regular physical presence or other objective and verifiable criteria), and Art. 45(6) bars meeting that condition through predetermined investments in Gibraltar's economy or real estate or predetermined payments to the Gibraltar authorities; Art. 50: before issuing or renewing a residence permit Gibraltar notifies Spain, which has 28 days (extendable by 14) to object on public policy, internal security, public health or international relations grounds (including a SIS alert), silence counting as no objection; proposed changes to the residence conditions go to the Cooperation Council for an opinion (Art. 45(4)). Domestic residence rules are the Residency Regulations 2026 (see Residence Criteria entry) | UK-EU Agreement MS No.9/2026 vol I (gov.uk) + gov.uk treaty collection + OEIL 2026/0058(NLE) + HMGoG PR 148/2026 + Technical Notices 696/2026 and 778/2026 + Commons Library CBP-10980 — https://commonslibrary.parliament.uk/research-briefings/cbp-10980 | 2026-10-21 | 2026-12-15 | 1 | --visa --foreign-buyer --remote (GI)`
- `2026-07-14 | visa | Residence Criteria framework (announced HMGoG PR 466/2026, 17 Jun 2026: residence based on genuine economic contribution and regulatory compliance; residence alone does not automatically give access to wider social benefits or services reserved for Gibraltarian Status) IN FORCE as the Residency Regulations 2026 — Legal Notice 166 of 2026 (Gazette No. 5323, 9 Jul 2026; made under ss.13 + 43(7) of the Treaty on Gibraltar and the European Union Act 2026; corrigendum LN 175/2026 of 10 Jul 2026 fixes two typos only; no amending instrument on gibraltarlaws.gov.gi as of 2026-10-21), in operation 14 Jul 2026. Routes: employee, self-employed, student, Crown/HM forces, existing resident (IARA residence document + civilian registration card + 6 months' continuous lawful residence), permanent resident, plus Government discretion (reg. 15: good character + interests of Gibraltar, e.g. wider economic or reputational benefit without material demand on public resources); identity-card holders need no permit; no nationality criterion (HMGoG PR 682/2026). Employee and self-employed applicants must earn at least Gibraltar average gross annual earnings, be 55 or under (Chief Minister may waive either for important skills or a shortage sector; under-30 employee earnings waiver against an employer deposit) and show a purchase or rental agreement for a Gibraltar property used as their primary residence (may be conditional on the permit; not a vessel, not a holiday rental, rentals at least 12 months, bona fide). Permits last 1 year, renewable; fee £250, renewal £20 per person; £2,500 administrative penalty for residing without a valid permit; refusal where the Schengen authorities object (reg. 20); IARA ss.18-34 and Part IV repealed, Withdrawal Agreement rights saved. ANNOUNCED, NOT YET MADE: HMGoG PR 813/2026 (21 Oct 2026) says the Regulations will be amended to add a route for entirely self-sufficient people over 55 (private medical insurance, a financial contribution to the health system, at least six months a year in Gibraltar), to be published by end-October 2026 after consultation. Cat-2: HMGoG PR 469/2026 (18 Jun 2026) announced the fee rising from £1,233 to £5,000 and new-applicant minimum net wealth rising from £2M to £5M, with existing holders grandfathered and loss of the right to remain resident for a person who does not keep Cat-2 status. Only the fee is legislated: Qualifying (Category 2) Individuals (Amendment) Rules 2026, LN 127/2026 (Gazette No. 5316, 22 Jun 2026, in operation on publication), substitutes £5,000 for £1,233 in rule 8(1)(a) and changes nothing else. The consolidated Rules (22 Jun 2026) set no numeric net-worth figure (rule 6(a): substantial and sound financial standing), so the £5M is an administrative threshold per PR 469 only. The loss-of-residence limb is in neither LN 127/2026 nor LN 166/2026 — data not publicly available in legislation; verify at Gibraltar Finance Centre. NOT public: only the control body, the authorities named in the law and financial intermediaries and advisers under Art. 2 Abs. 3bis/3ter GwG may use it, for their legal duties; queries capped for now, full-register queries and discrepancy reports later. Each reporting legal entity registers once and then reports changes in its beneficial owners; deadlines run from one month to two years after 1 Oct 2026 (six months for foreign-law entities). Land: a foreign-law legal entity acquiring land within the meaning of Art. 4 BewG must prove its register entry when the purchase is filed with the Grundbuch; without it the land registry suspends the entry, sets a 10-day deadline and then rejects it (Art. 40 TJPG). Intentional reporting violations: fines up to CHF 500,000 (Art. 43 TJPG). AML: the revised GwG puts due-diligence duties on advisers who professionally take part in financial transactions tied to buying or selling land (Art. 2 Abs. 3bis lit. a GwG), lawyers and notaries included subject to the GwV thresholds; Amtsnotariate not yet covered | admin.ch Medienmitteilung 1 Oct 2026 https://www.admin.ch/de/newnsb/L0rkU9ZvCFSw + bj.admin.ch https://www.bj.admin.ch/de/schweizerisches-transparenzregister + Fedlex SR 955.3 https://www.fedlex.admin.ch/eli/cc/2026/323/de | 2026-10-21 | 2027-04-01 | 3 | --sanctions --notary --foreign-buyer (CH)`

- `2024-XX-XX | tax | No foreign-buyer quota exists in Tirol/Salzburg/Vorarlberg — two distinct regimes were conflated. (1) Non-EU/EEA acquisition is an approval regime with no quota or percentage cap. CORRECTION on the deciding authority, which differs by Land. Tirol: the Grundverkehrsbehörde is the Bezirksverwaltungsbehörde (§ 26 TGVG 1996); approval (§ 12) is granted only if the acquisition does not conflict with staatspolitische Interessen and serves a public economic, cultural or social interest (§ 13 Abs 1 lit c). Salzburg: Zustimmung by the Grundverkehrsbeauftragte(r), or the Grundverkehrskommission for farm or forest land (§§ 20 Abs 3, 24, 25 S.GVG 2023, LGBl 95/2022). Vorarlberg: the Grundverkehrs-Landeskommission decides for non-farm land (§§ 7, 8, 13 Abs 2 GVG). EU/EEA: Tirol equates EU/EEA nationals and Art 50 EUV residence-title holders with Austrians outright (§ 3 Abs 1 TGVG). Salzburg (§ 22 S.GVG, declaration required) and Vorarlberg (§ 3 GVG) exempt acquisitions made in exercise of EU/EEA freedoms including free movement of capital; in Vorarlberg the capital route needs EU/EEA residence. (2) The caps that do exist are nationality-neutral second-home caps. Tirol: new Freizeitwohnsitze may not be declared permissible once registered Freizeitwohnsitze plus those allowed by Flächenwidmungsplan designation exceed 8 v.H. of total dwellings per the last Gebäude- und Wohnungszählung (§ 13 Abs 5 lit a TROG 2022, version in force 7 Jul 2026 per LGBl 51/2026). Salzburg: Zweitwohnung-Beschränkungsgemeinden are communes where non-Hauptwohnsitz dwellings exceed 16 % of the total stock (§ 31 Abs 1 Z 1 ROG 2009, unchanged since 1 Mar 2023, LGBl 95/2022). They are designated by Landesregierung Verordnung every FIVE years on the five-year arithmetic mean at Stichtag 31 Oct, not annually, and a Gemeinde may apply to have its percentage raised. No tightening through 21 Oct 2026. CORRECTION: Tirol LGBl 51/2026 (Gesetz vom 1 Jul 2026, kundgemacht 6 Jul 2026) is a building/energy omnibus, but its TROG article also amended § 13. It reworded the Abs 1 definition and restructured the Abs 8 Bürgermeister-Ausnahmebewilligung grounds, adding one for lifetime gifts to statutory heirs where the donor kept a residential servitude until death; the 8 v.H. cap is unchanged. Salzburg ROG amendment LGBl 79/2026 (Gesetz vom 8 Jul 2026, kundgemacht 31 Jul 2026) touches only §§ 5 Z 2, 53 Abs 2 Z 11, 60 Abs 1 und 5 and 88 Abs 9. TGVG 1996 (last amended LGBl 72/2025), S.GVG 2023 (last LGBl 75/2025) and the Vorarlberg GVG (last LGBl 44/2025) have no 2026 amendment (2026 data, RIS konsolidiertes Landesrecht Fassung vom 21.10.2026) | RIS konsolidiertes Landesrecht Tirol (TGVG 1996, TROG 2022) / Salzburg (S.GVG 2023, ROG 2009) / Vorarlberg (GVG) — https://www.ris.bka.gv.at/NormDokument.wxe?Abfrage=LrSbg&Gesetzesnummer=20000615&Paragraf=31 | 2026-10-21 | 2027-01-19 | 2 | --tax (ownership)`

FMSG still expected banks to keep the KIM-V criteria, and FMA chose a Rundschreiben as the instrument: the FMA WIK-Rundschreiben on solide Vergabe von privaten Wohnimmobilienkrediten (published 26 Jun 2025) keeps max 90% Beleihungsquote, max 40% of annual net income as Schuldendienstquote and max 35-year term as a Richtwert. It is NOT binding: a bank may deviate if its risk strategy justifies a higher risk appetite. FMSG 49th session (25 Jun 2026): the share of new private residential loans outside the FMSG criteria has risen since KIM-V ended but stayed below 20% of new lending at aggregate level, monitoring continues. CORRECTION: two distinct share-deal rules, both now in § 1 Abs 3 GrEStG for Personen- AND Kapitalgesellschaften. (a) Gesellschafterwechsel: at least 75% of the shares pass directly to new shareholders within SEVEN years (old § 1 Abs 2a covered partnerships only, 95% within five years). (b) Anteilsvereinigung / Anteilsübertragung: direct or indirect unification or transfer of at least 75% (old 95%) in one acquirer or acquirer group, with no observation period. CORRECTION on the rate (§ 7 Abs 1 Z 2 lit c): 3.5% of gemeiner Wert ONLY where the company is an Immobiliengesellschaft (§ 4 Abs 4: business focus on selling, letting or managing land); all other share deals stay at 0.5% of Grundstückswert (pre-reform 0.5% for all). ImmoESt § 30 Abs 6a EStG (sales after 30 Jun 2025): the gain on land rezoned after 31 Dec 2024 is raised by a 30% Umwidmungszuschlag, capped at the sale proceeds, before the 30% rate. Unchanged (no GrEStG amendment after BGBl I 97/2025): GrESt Stufentarif for unentgeltliche Erwerbe incl. Familienverband (0.5% first EUR 250,000 / 2% next EUR 150,000 / 3.5% above, § 7 Abs 1 Z 2 lit a) and the 3.5% standard rate. FOLLOW-ON (gazetted, applies to sales after 31 Dec 2026): Budgetbegleitgesetz 2027-2028 (BGBl I 62/2026) cuts the flat-rate acquisition cost for Altvermögen in § 30 Abs 4 EStG from 86% to 80% of proceeds (rezoned after 1987: 40% to 30%), so effective ImmoESt on old property rises from 4.2% to 6.0% of proceeds and on rezoned old land from 18% to 21% (computed: 30% x 20% / 30% x 70%) | RIS GrEStG consolidated (Fassung vom 21.10.2026 and 30.06.2025) + RIS EStG §§ 30 / 124b + BMF Grunderwerbsteuer Steuersatz — https://www.ris.bka.gv.at/GeltendeFassung.wxe?Abfrage=Bundesnormen&Gesetzesnummer=10004531 | 2026-10-21 | 2027-01-19 | 2 | --tax (AT)`

PROPOSED, NOT ADOPTED: the Belastingplan 2027 bill (Kamerstuk 37 022, Artikel XXI onderdeel A, presented on Prinsjesdag 15 Sep 2026) cuts it from 8% to 7% from 1 Jan 2027. Unchanged: the 2% owner-occupier rate, the 10.4% general rate on other immovable property (commercial premises, undeveloped land, a garage not bought with the home) and the legal terms of the startersvrijstelling (its 2027 ceiling of EUR 615,000 is a separate, gazetted indexation — see the startersvrijstelling entry). Status 29 Oct 2026: not yet voted — the Tweede Kamer plenary debate on the Belastingplan 2027 is planned for week 45 (2-6 Nov 2026; tweedekamer.nl lists the week, not a day); rijksoverheid.nl puts the Tweede Kamer and Eerste Kamer votes in November and December 2026 (Tweede Kamer vote listed for 12 Nov 2026 in the Kamer planning — verify at tweedekamer.nl); the Finance Ministry summary says the plans become final at the end of December. The nota's van wijziging of 14 and 20 Oct 2026 (Kamerstuk 37 022 nrs. 17 and 22) leave the ovb rate unchanged, no amendment filed up to nr. 24 (20 Oct 2026) concerns overdrachtsbelasting, and the nota naar aanleiding van het verslag (nr. 21, 20 Oct 2026) keeps 1 Jan 2027 with no retroactive effect. The 8% rate applies to deeds passed in 2026; if adopted, the BTL / second-home buyer-side total (~10–11%) falls by about 1 point for deeds from 1 Jan 2027. Announced by NHG 13 Oct 2026; laid down in the NHG Voorwaarden en Normen in force 1 Jan 2027. Method unchanged since 2023: average purchase prices over the past 27 months plus 5%, rounded to a multiple of EUR 5,000. NHG schedules publication of the V&N 2027 on nhg.nl for 1 Nov 2026 (the V&N page still showed 2026-1 on 2026-10-28); intended changes listed by NHG, final text pending: a foundation-risk test (a verkennend funderingsonderzoek is mandatory where the valuation gives funderingsrisico-inschatting D or E, and where it shows high risk the indicative repair costs reduce the allowed financieringslast) and, for E, F or G-label homes other than apartments, a default of at least EUR 20,000 in the NHG loan for energy-saving measures, which the buyer may decline by declaration. Re-check the published V&N 2027 | NHG https://www.nhg.nl/nhg-actueel/nhg-grens-in-2027-naar-500000-euro + NHG V&N 2027 planning https://www.nhg.nl/kennis-innovatie/voorwaarden-en-normen-2027/ | 2026-10-28 | 2026-11-02 | 4 | --finance`

- `pending (Sejm passed 2026-10-08; Senate committees met late Oct 2026; Senate vote due Nov 2026) | rental | BILL PASSED BY THE SEJM, NOT IN FORCE — Poland's act implementing EU Reg 2024/1028 is the government bill amending the ustawa z dnia 29 sierpnia 1997 r. o usługach hotelarskich oraz usługach pilotów wycieczek i przewodników turystycznych (Sejm print 2865): adopted by the Rada Ministrów 14 Jul 2026, received by the Sejm 21 Jul 2026, first reading 2 Sep 2026, passed 8 Oct 2026 (407 for, 24 against, 9 abstaining), then sent to the Senate (Senate print 863 of 9 Oct 2026 per a senat.gov.pl listing; the page was not reachable to confirm). Not in force: the Sejm process record (last change 2026-10-09, ELI null) shows no later stage, no Senate position or presidential signature was found, and Dz.U. 2026 through poz. 1417 (promulgated 28 Oct 2026) carries no such act (checked 2026-10-29). Senate stage (press, as senat.gov.pl was not reachable): three joint Senate committees took up the act in late October 2026 and amendment proposals from that debate go to a Senate vote in November, among them senator Górska's proposal that a spółdzielnia may ban STR by a simple majority of members present at the general meeting; the Senate Legislative Bureau also proposed deleting the wspólnota's power to ban STR, and the report says the senators did not take up the Bureau's constitutional doubts; none adopted yet (superbiz.se.pl, 27 Oct 2026). Under art. 121(2) of the Constitution the Senate has 30 days from transmission to accept, amend or reject the act; if it passes no resolution in that time, the Sejm text is deemed adopted (about 8 Nov 2026 if the act was transmitted on the 9 Oct print date, an assumption, as that date is unconfirmed). Text sent to the Senate: usługi hotelarskie are lets of less than 30 days at a time to one client (art. 3 ust. 1 pkt 8); a public Centralny Wykaz Turystycznych Obiektów Noclegowych (CWTON) on the tourism minister's website (art. 42 ust. 4), with the gmina head as the designated authority under art. 15(2) of the Regulation (art. 42 ust. 5) and the tourism minister as national coordinator and single digital entry point (art. 44d); the host gives the platform the CWTON ID before listing (art. 39 ust. 3a); the mandatory regulamin porządkowy states the ID and a contact person's name and phone number (art. 44 ust. 3); administrative fines up to PLN 50,000 for operating without an ewidencja entry (art. 45e) or for offers without the ID (art. 45f ust. 2), and up to PLN 50,000 per platform breach of Reg 2024/1028 arts 6, 7(2)-(3) and 9 after an unanswered 7-day notice (art. 45g); removal from the ewidencja after 3 safety or public-order violations within 6 months of the first, with no re-entry for 1 year (art. 41b). Timing: most provisions 14 days after Dz.U. publication (art. 8); gminas pass existing data to CWTON within 3 months (art. 5); existing ewidencja entries are adapted within 4 months or lapse (art. 6); from 1 Jan 2028 a wspólnota (owners' resolution) or a spółdzielnia mieszkaniowa (general assembly, majority above 50% of those entitled to vote) may ban STR in its property, with 6 months for the host to stop (art. 39b), and a rada gminy may set zones where STR is excluded (art. 44b: consultation of at least 21 days, resolution effective no earlier than 6 months after publication). The Senate may still amend any of this. CORRECTION: no 15 Oct 2026 gmina deadline appears in the bill or in the MSiT release (superseded); the government bill was not in the Sejm in April 2026 — the only short-let bill then was the poselski projekt ustawy o najmie krótkoterminowym (print 2353, letter dated 3 Dec 2025, first reading 17 Apr 2026, in committee, not passed). MinFin posted the 90th-TIF bill «Ενίσχυση του διαθέσιμου εισοδήματος, της αποταμίευσης και των επενδύσεων, φορολογικές, μισθολογικές, συνταξιοδοτικές διατάξεις και λοιπές διατάξεις κοινωνικής πολιτικής» for public consultation on 18 Oct 2026 until 1 Nov 2026, aiming to legislate it within November 2026; its release gives the 1 Jul 2027 start and the non-EU/EEA tax-residence test, and the MinFin 2027 draft State Budget (Oct 2026) gives the 15% / 15.45% rate and books +€41m for the part-year 2027 (the €100m/yr in the September 2026 government measures table is a full-year figure). SCOPE CHANGED from the announcement: the test is tax residence, not citizenship, so the announcement's citizenship / first-home-beneficiary wording (Greeks, ομογενείς, EU/EEA citizens, long-term residents) is superseded. Draft Art. 16 (opengov consultation text, read 2026-10-28) adds par. 1A to art. 27 of the Property Tax Code (L.5219/2025): 15% on the taxable value when a dwelling is bought by a natural person who, «κατά τον χρόνο γένεσης της φορολογικής υποχρέωσης» (when the tax liability arises), is tax-resident in a country outside the EU and EEA. It has no citizenship test, and the 48-article draft has no carve-out for ομογενείς, long-term residents or Golden Visa buyers, so a Greek citizen or ομογενής tax-resident outside the EU/EEA would pay 15% and a non-EU national tax-resident in Greece or another EU/EEA state 3%. Draft Art. 48(8) applies it «για οριστικά συμβόλαια που καταρτίζονται από την 1η.7.2027 και μετά» (to final contracts drawn up from 1 Jul 2027) and says nothing about preliminary contracts signed earlier. It does not address Property Tax Code art. 26 par. 1(a), under which a final contract signed within two years of a notarial preliminary contract is taxed «με βάση τους συντελεστές που ίσχυαν κατά τον χρόνο σύνταξης του προσυμφώνου» (at the rates in force when the preliminary was signed), so whether a notarial preliminary signed before 1 Jul 2027 keeps a non-EU/EEA-resident buyer at 3% is unresolved in the draft. The wording can still change after the consultation. Not tabled, voted or gazetted as of 2026-10-28 (Hellenic Parliament tabled-bills list, newest item 26 Oct 2026, a different bill; et.gr ΦΕΚ Α 2026 full-text search for the Art. 16 wording: no hit), so 3.09% remains the current rate; confirm the enacted Art. 16 text (scope, any carve-out, transitional rule) on the Hellenic Parliament bill page / ΦΕΚ | minfin.gov.gr 90th-TIF bill press release 18 Oct 2026 https://minfin.gov.gr/anartithike-se-dimosia-diavoulefsi-to-nomoschedio-me-ta-metra-pou-anakoinothikan-stin-90i-diethni-ekthesi-thessalonikis-kai-efarmozontai-kata-ta-eti-2026-kai-2027/ + MinFin 2027 draft State Budget (p.51 + Table 2.5) `https://minfin.gov.gr/wp-content/uploads/2026/10/%CE%A0%CE%A1%CE%9F%CE%A3%CE%A7%CE%95%CE%94%CE%99%CE%9F-%CE%9A%CE%A1%CE%91%CE%A4%CE%99%CE%9A%CE%9F%CE%A5-%CE%A0%CE%A1%CE%9F%CE%AB%CE%A0%CE%9F%CE%9B%CE%9F%CE%93%CE%99%CE%A3%CE%9C%CE%9F%CE%A5-2027-1.pdf` + government.gov.gr 90th TIF measures https://www.government.gov.gr/exidikefsi-ton-ikonomikon-metron-pou-anakinose-prothipourgos-stin-90i-deth + AADE FMA page (current 3%) https://www.aade.gr/omogeneis-katoikoi-exoterikoy/kefalaio/forologia-metabibasis-akiniton-fma + opengov.gr consultation text (draft Arts 16 and 48(8)) `https://opengov.gr/minfin/deliberations/enischysi-toy-diathesimoy-eisodimatos-tis-apotamieysis-kai-ton-ependyseon-forologikes-misthologikes/` + Property Tax Code art. 26 (preliminary-contract rate rule) via taxheaven `https://www.taxheaven.gr/law/5219/2025/arthro/26` + Hellenic Parliament tabled bills `https://www.hellenicparliament.gr/Nomothetiko-Ergo/Katatethenta-Nomosxedia` | 2026-10-28 | 2026-11-16 | 1 | --tax --notary --visa --compare (GR)`

- `2025-01-01 | rental | Athens 1st/2nd/3rd Municipal Districts AMA freeze: no first-time STR registrations under L.4446/2016 art.111 par.2A (added by L.5162/2024 art.29). The statute's 1st sentence sets 1 Jan–31 Dec 2025; KYA 225563 ΕΞ 2025 (ΦΕΚ Β' 7200/31-12-2025) EXTENDED it to 31 Dec 2026. Hosts registered before 2024-12-31 continue (two-tier market). Unlawful STR fine = 50% of STR income, min €20,000; a repeat breach in the same tax year = rents collected, min €40,000. L.5313/2026 art.5 (ΦΕΚ 102/Α/25-6-2026) replaced the words 'μέχρι την 31η Δεκεμβρίου 2026' in par.2A's 5th sentence, which now says only that the par.8 joint ministerial decision may extend the suspension, so there is no longer a statutory end-date cap. The same article added par.2B and par.2Γ. Par.2B: Thessaloniki A' Municipal Community first-registration freeze 1 Jul–31 Dec 2026, same fines, also extendable by a par.8 KYA. Par.2Γ: AMA deletion ENACTED — a property in the par.2A / 2B zones transferred inter vivos for any cause, onerous or gratuitous, is deleted from the STR register and cannot re-register while the first-registration freeze lasts; inheritance is outside the text; par.2Γ in force from publication on 25 Jun 2026 under L.5313/2026 art.137 par.1 (art.5 is not among its exceptions). The PM ANNOUNCED a 2027 extension (Athens + Thessaloniki to 31 Dec 2027) at the 90th TIF (Sep 2026, news-tier); the MinFin 2027 draft State Budget and DBP 2027 (Oct 2026) list it as a planned 2027 measure ('και για το έτος 2027'). - `2026-07-24 | rental | ENACTED — 5-year short-term-letting ban on homes created through the new exceptional change-of-use routes. L.5322/2026 art. 56(2) (ΦΕΚ Α' 117/24.07.2026; in force on publication, art. 99) bars short-term letting under art. 111 par. 1 L.4446/2016 of the dwellings «του τελευταίου εδαφίου της παρ. 3 και της παρ. 3Α του άρθρου 199» of the Spatial Planning-Urban Planning Code (ν. 5306/2026) «για διάστημα τουλάχιστον πέντε (5) ετών από τη συντέλεση της αλλαγής χρήσης σε κατοικία» ('for at least five years from completion of the change of use to housing'). Both routes were added by art. 56(1): par. 3, conversion to housing of buildings inside an approved town plan or settlement, by exception and regardless of the area's land uses, including buildings given a building-terms derogation up to 31 Dec 2025 (the ban cites par. 3's last sentence, the Central Architecture Council opinion attached to this conversion); par. 3A, conversion to housing of lawfully existing out-of-plan buildings regardless of land uses, building terms and special spatial regimes, once only, inside the existing shell, with no added volume or coverage. A change of use that the land-use rules already permit (par. 2 and the first three sentences of par. 3) is outside the ban, so not every office-to-flat conversion is caught. Art. 56 sets no penalty of its own and the ban is not written into art. 111 L.4446/2016 itself; the sanction route and AADE AMA handling are not verified. Buyer check: on a recently converted flat or out-of-plan house, get the change-of-use permit file; no STR for at least 5 years after the conversion was completed | ΦΕΚ Α' 117/24.07.2026 (National Printing House) `https://ia37rg02wpsa01.blob.core.windows.net/fek/01/2026/20260100117.pdf` + codified text via taxheaven `https://www.taxheaven.gr/law/5322/2026/arthro/56` + `https://www.taxheaven.gr/law/5306/2026/arthro/199` | 2026-10-28 | 2027-04-26 | 3 | --rental`

Breach → residence-permit revocation + €50,000 standalone administrative fine on owners/holders (Law 5100/2024 Art. 64, adding para 7A to Art. 100 of Immigration Code Law 5038/2023). - `2027-01-01 | tax | ENACTED — municipal Local Development Levy (Τέλος Τοπικής Ανάπτυξης, ΤΤΑ) replaces the Τέλος Ακίνητης Περιουσίας (ΤΑΠ, art. 24 L.2130/1993) and the Φόρος Ηλεκτροδοτούμενων Χώρων (ΦΗΧ, art. 10 L.1080/1980) from 1 Jan 2027: ΤΑΠ and ΦΗΧ apply until 31 Dec 2026, and the ΤΤΑ applies to electricity-bill consumption periods starting on or after 1 Jan 2027 (Local Government Code Ν.5314/2026, ΦΕΚ Α΄ 103/29.06.2026, art. 448). Each municipal council sets the rate at 0.30‰–0.70‰ of property value, variable by municipal unit; a higher rate only to reach 95% of the municipality's 2025 ΤΑΠ+ΦΗΧ revenue; value = m² × zone price × age coefficient under the L.5219/2025 objective-value system (art. 393). Owed by the owner (or usufructuary / possessor) as of 1 January, split by co-ownership share; a new owner files an e-declaration with the municipality within one month (art. 394). Collected on the electricity bill for metered properties; where the bill is in a tenant's name the tenant pays it and deducts it from the rent unless the lease says otherwise (art. 395). At sale no municipal clearance certificate is required: the ΤΑΠ non-debt certificate was repealed from 13 Dec 2023 (art. 33 par. 1 Ν.5076/2023), and under the new Code the Hellenic Cadastre notifies the municipality within 10 days of registration, with ΤΤΑ owed up to the transfer date borne by the seller (art. 392). - `2027-01-01 (planned district rules; only VI and VII zero-day rules adopted as of 2026-10-28) | rental | Budapest districts' STR rules for the period after the Kertv. 11/H moratorium (status as of 2026-10-28). Zero-day rules adopted only in District VI (decree 26/2024 (X.31.)) and District VII (decree 46/2025 (XII.10.), zero days without a társasház agreement) — see their entries; no other district zero-day decree adopted (absence of evidence plus a secondary roundup of late Aug 2026); District I published a draft one on 22 Oct 2026 (below). District V (Belváros-Lipótváros): a CAP, not a ban — egyéb and magánszálláshely units limited to 5% of a building's independent units in the Vt-V/1 and Vt-V/2 zones (KÉSZ 6/2020 (I.30.) §75(3) and §76(4)); the Kúria rejected a challenge in Köf.5.068/2024/3 of 18 Feb 2025; amending decree 2/2026 (II.12.), in force 1 Apr 2026, restated the 5% cap as a combined count, set minimum room sizes (12 m² single room, 18 m² for two or more beds, plus 6 m² per bed from the third), banned converting premises into panzió or közösségi szálláshely and limited registrations under the old rules to 30 Jun 2027. District VIII (Józsefváros): PROPOSED, not adopted — concept adopted by resolution 124/2026 (VI.25.): district cap 3.5% of the housing stock (actual share 3.52% in Mar 2026, so in practice no new units), sub-district caps 1% to 5% (5% in Palotanegyed and Corvin), per-building cap 15% by floor area (the consultation draft had 10-20%), condominium consent for new units; the JÓKÉSZ 4th amendment went to partnership consultation in Sep 2026; the companion condominium-consent decree (draft titled for nem lakás célú helyiségek, i.e. non-residential premises, in condominiums: indefinite consent needed, revocable with reasons from the first day of the fourth month after the condominium's decision; consent presumed for units registered before 1 Jan 2027) was tabled for the 24 Sep 2026 council meeting and withdrawn by its proposers, and neither item was on the 22 Oct 2026 agenda; the withdrawn proposal expected the JÓKÉSZ amendment at the Nov 2026 meeting, both to enter into force 1 Jan 2027. District XIII: preparing a decree allowing STR only with a condominium or cooperative assembly resolution (draft published, survey responses being processed per the district page last modified 5 Oct 2026; no adoption found as of 2026-10-28; 1,100-1,200 STR flats per the district). District I (Budavár): PROPOSED zero-day decree under Kertv. 12. § (5a), published for 15-day consultation on 22 Oct 2026 after a non-binding poll (58.6% of 4,503 respondents for a full ban): 0 usable days district-wide from 1 Jan 2027, except Várnegyed units registered by 31 Dec 2026 (365 days to 31 Dec 2027 only), units outside the Várnegyed in non-condominium buildings (365) and units outside the Várnegyed in condominium-type buildings (társasház, lakásszövetkezet or co-owned building of 3+ flats) registered by 31 Dec 2026 (365 to 31 Dec 2027, then 365 only from the day condominium consent is filed with the jegyző); the draft gives units registered from 2027 in condominium-type buildings no exception; fine HUF 200,000 (natural person / sole trader) or HUF 2,000,000 (legal person) plus a 45-day closure, set without discretion; council decision expected Nov 2026. - `2026-12-15 (proposed — bill submitted to the Országgyűlés 2026-10-27; T/ number not confirmed; not adopted as of 2026-10-28) | tax | Hungary wealth tax (vagyonadó) — PROPOSED, NOT ADOPTED. The Pénzügyminisztérium published the draft act (vagyonadóról szóló törvényjavaslat) for public consultation on 6 Oct 2026; consultation closed 14 Oct 2026; the government submitted the bill to the Országgyűlés on the evening of 27 Oct 2026 (Portfolio, Index, Jogászvilág — secondary; parlament.hu sits behind a CAPTCHA, so the T/ number and stage are not confirmed). Per Portfolio's draft-vs-bill comparison (28 Oct 2026) the bill keeps the HUF 1bn allowance, the 1% / 1.5% rates and the dates below and changes it on 16 main points; for property: debts directly tied to acquiring, building, maintaining or improving the property become deductible inside a property-holding company before a non-resident's share is pro-rated; a taxpayer who uses the NAV mass-valuation model with the property's real parameters can be assessed on a different value only if NAV proves the parameters were wrong; the model now also covers holiday homes (üdülő) and plots (telek); a valuer whose breach yields under 70% of market value is fined HUF 100,000. Draft terms: annual tax on net wealth above HUF 1bn (the allowance applies to each spouse separately) at 1% up to HUF 100bn and 1.5% above; HU residents are taxed on their whole wealth; non-residents only on Hungarian real estate, rights over or linked to it, shares in companies formed under Hungarian law and shares in any company holding Hungarian real estate (draft s. 4(2)), deducting only documented loans tied to acquiring, building, maintaining or improving those assets. Hungarian property is valued in a fixed order: a transaction value from the last 12 months; else an older transaction value (up to 10 years) indexed with the NAV calculator; else the NAV national mass-valuation figure; the taxpayer may opt for an independent valuer's market value, and a registered valuer's figure of HUF 500m or more must be replaced by a court-registered (judicial) expert's valuation. The draft has no main-residence exemption, and Portfolio's list of bill changes adds none. Status is taken on 31 Dec; self-assessed return and payment by 31 Aug of the following year; NAV may grant deferral or instalments for liquidity hardship, normally up to 12 months and exceptionally up to 24 months, especially for tax on housing property or company shares. Draft entry into force 15 Dec 2026, first status date 31 Dec 2026, first return and payment by 31 Aug 2027. The MoF impact sheet (PM/4400/2026) estimates 15,000 individual and 2,000 trust-type taxpayers. Confirm the enacted text (T/ number on parlament.hu, then promulgation in Magyar Közlöny) before relying on these figures | Pénzügyminisztérium consultation page + tartalmi összefoglaló — https://kormany.hu/dokumentumtar/tarsadalmi-egyeztetes-a-vagyonadorol-szolo-torvenyjavaslatrol + https://kormany.hu/application/documents/6025c5a8-77c8-476c-a3f3-a05d86d7b9bf/download + Portfolio 28 Oct 2026 (bill vs draft) — https://www.portfolio.hu/gazdasag/20261028/vagyonado-benyujtotta-a-kormany-a-torvenyjavaslatot-mutatjuk-mi-valtozott-872400 + Index 28 Oct 2026 — https://index.hu/gazdasag/2026/10/28/vagyonado-adobevallas-vagyonnyilatkozat-kormany-torvenytervezet-karman-andras-penzugyminiszterium/ | 2026-10-29 | 2026-11-20 | 3 | --tax`

- `2025-09-25 | rental | Budapest District VII (Erzsébetváros) two-stage STR crackdown. Building decree 33/2025 (IX.24), amending Építési Szabályzat 25/2018 (XII.21), in force 25 Sept 2025 (its § 17: day after promulgation): commercial accommodation in residential buildings, expressly including short-term letting, allowed only on max 10% of the building's residential usable floor area, on the ground floor only, with rooms of min 8 m² single / 12 m² double / +4 m² per extra bed and an en-suite bathroom (WC, washbasin, shower) per room; a panzió only with ≥6 rooms + breakfast room; non-business community/leisure accommodation barred from residential buildings. The decree has no transitional clause — per THT (secondary) it applies to new authorisation procedures, so operating units may continue. THEN decree 46/2025 (XII.10), in force 1 Jan 2026 and applied to pending cases: STR usable days/yr = 0 for magánszálláshely and egyéb szálláshely UNLESS a condominium (társasház) agreement exists (then 365) — a general-meeting resolution, SZMSZ provision or founding-deed clause clearly referring to the accommodation; fine HUF 200,000 (natural person / sole trader) or HUF 2,000,000 (legal person) or a 45-day closure, set without discretion; units registered by 31 Dec 2025 must obtain and file the agreement by 31 Dec 2026 — same zero-day mechanism as District VI (decree 26/2024 X.31, upheld by Kúria Köf.5.023/2025/4 of 11 Nov 2025), with a condo-consent carve-out. Budapest-wide moratorium (Kertv. 11/H, inserted by Act LVI of 2024, in force 1 Jan 2025; text in force from 1 Oct 2026 unchanged): no new magánszálláshely or egyéb szálláshely registered until 31 Dec 2026 (exception: classification request filed by 31 Dec 2024), and for units registered by 31 Dec 2024 no change of operator registered until 31 Dec 2026 | Erzsébetváros decrees 33/2025 + 46/2025 + consolidated 25/2018 via net.jogtar.hu + Kertv. 11/H via net.jogtar.hu + Kúria Köf.5.023/2025/4 — https://net.jogtar.hu/rendelet?council=erzsebetvaros&dbnum=560&docid=A2500046.07R | 2026-10-21 | 2027-01-19 | 1 | --rental`
- `2025-01-01 | rental | Budapest STR registration moratorium — Kertv. (2005. évi CLXIV. törvény) 11/H. §, inserted by 2024. évi LVI. törvény 20. §, in force since 1 Jan 2025: a national statute that applies only in Budapest's administrative area, not a municipal decree. (1) Until 31 Dec 2026 the kereskedelmi hatóság (the district jegyző) may not register any new magánszálláshely or egyéb szálláshely; (2) the only exemption covers operators who submitted the szálláshely-minősítési (classification) request by 31 Dec 2024, i.e. completed the self-assessment on the classification body's portal; (3) for units registered by 31 Dec 2024 a change in the person of the szálláshely-szolgáltató (operator) cannot be registered until 31 Dec 2026 — a buyer of a registered Budapest STR unit cannot be registered as its operator before 1 Jan 2027. Units registered by 31 Dec 2024 are outside the ban because they are not new and keep operating. The consolidated text in force from 1 Oct 2026 shows no extension (net.jogtar.hu time state 2026.X.1. with no end date, read 2026-10-28); host interest groups asked the MTÜ for a one-year extension (secondary report of 14 Sep 2026), and four host associations (MAKE, FATOSZ, FMKE, FSZKSZ) asked in a joint position paper for an extension to 31 Dec 2027 plus repeal of the municipalities' power to cap letting days and of the caps already adopted (turizmus.com report of 14 Oct 2026, secondary). No extension bill found by title as of 2026-10-28 (pending-bill list on torvenyfigyelo.hu, synced from parlament.hu 27 Oct 2026): the only pending Kertv. amendment is government bill T/937 of 27 Oct 2026, titled 'A kereskedelemről szóló 2005. évi CLXIV. törvény módosításáról', which matches the bill economx reports as ending the food and drugstore margin cap (secondary); its text sits behind a parlament.hu CAPTCHA and was not read, so whether it also touches 11/H is UNVERIFIED. - `pending (announced 2026-10-19 for 2027; no decree as of 2026-10-21) | finance | Wekerle Plusz purchase and construction loan ANNOUNCED, NOT LEGISLATED: on 19 Oct 2026, in a pre-agenda speech to the National Assembly outlining the 2027 budget, the Prime Minister said it replaces the Otthon Start Program in 2027. Announced terms (news only): applicants aged 18-41 (one report says under 41, so the upper boundary is unclear); fixed 3% for buying or building a new home, fixed 5% for a used home; a 5-year duty to live in the home; neither the applicant nor the spouse may hold a share reaching 50% in a dwelling; Otthon Start contracts already signed stay unchanged. Loan cap, term, price limits and launch date are not yet published; no decree exists, and the 2027 budget bill had not been submitted as of 20 Oct 2026 (jogihirek.hu, secondary). The Otthon Start closing date is unsettled: vg.hu reports a year-end 2026 closure, while e-ingatlanugyvedek.hu and other secondary sources say no date has been published. Otthon Start baseline under Korm. rendelet 227/2025. (VII. 31.) (njt.hu; applications from 1 Sep 2025): rate capped at 3%, loan up to HUF 50M, term up to 25 years, price caps HUF 100M for a flat and HUF 150M for a single-dwelling house plus HUF 1.5M per m², a two-year Hungarian social-security eligibility period, no age cap and no duty to live in the home; not strictly first-time buyers — under § 4(4) an applicant may have held a share of at most 50% in at most one dwelling within the past 10 years. Confidence LOW (news only) | telex.hu 2026-10-19 — https://telex.hu/gazdasag/2026/10/19/otthon-start-helyett-wekerle-program-kinek-hany-eves-korig-mennyi-energetikai-korszerusites-kiknek-reszletek + https://www.vg.hu/vilaggazdasag-magyar-gazdasag/2026/10/lakaspiac-otthon-start-koltsegvetes-2027-wekerle-plusz + https://e-ingatlanugyvedek.hu/blog/az-otthon-start-program-meddig-tart (all secondary) | 2026-10-21 | 2026-12-15 | 4 | --finance`

- `2025-07-03 | ownership | RU/BY purchase ban — Nacionālo drošību apdraudošu darījumu ierobežošanas likums (adopted by the Saeima 19 Jun 2025, proclaimed 2 Jul 2025, Latvijas Vēstnesis 124, in force 3 Jul 2025; unamended in the likumi.lv text archived 22 May 2026) — a national-security statute, not an EU sanctions measure (preventing circumvention of RU/BY sanctions is one stated purpose, Art. 1(5)). Barred from acquiring Latvian real estate or a share of it (Art. 2): Russia and Belarus; their citizens; entities registered there; entities 25% owned by RU/BY-registered entities; entities at least 25% held directly or indirectly by RU/BY nationals or with RU/BY-citizen beneficial owners; legal arrangements set up in RU/BY or with RU/BY-citizen beneficial owners. Acquisitions after 3 Jul 2025 are void and cannot be registered in the Land Book; no auction bids, pre-emption or buy-out rights (Art. 3). Exceptions for RU/BY citizens only (Art. 4(1)): a final court ruling settling an ownership dispute (not a court-approved settlement); inheritance by a spouse or relatives up to the 4th degree; ending compulsory split ownership (dalītais īpašums) by adding the land share that goes with their apartment; a deal fulfilling Latvia's international obligations; one sole dwelling for a citizen with EU long-term-resident status in Latvia or a valid permanent residence permit issued by 3 Jul 2025. Other heirs receive the sale proceeds via a notary-appointed curator (Art. 4(3)).
