From 1 Oct 2026 new homes sold by VAT-registered developers carry the 21% standard rate (economedia and Digi24, 1 Oct 2026, secondary); no further extension enacted as of 2026-10-28 (legislatie.just.ro lists no actions undergone by Legea 161/2026, and the acts adopted at the 20 and 23 Oct 2026 government meetings include no VAT measure). Refund: Legea 161/2026 art. II lets an individual who paid the standard rate on qualifying homes bought between 1 Aug 2026 and the law's entry into force reclaim the difference down to 9% for one home (the one whose VAT chargeable event came first), claimable from 1 Oct 2026 under an ANAF president's order due within 30 days of entry into force; same-tier secondary sources disagree on whether 7 Aug 2026 itself is covered (PwC 1-7 Aug, avocatnet 1-6 Aug), so quote the statutory wording; no such ANAF order located on 2026-10-28 (legislatie.just.ro lists only promulgation Decree 712/2026 as referring to Legea 161/2026). ANNOUNCED, NOT LAW: a one-off 12-month extension to 31 Jul 2027 for homes contracted by authenticated deed by 31 Dec 2025, with no further extensions, was in PM-designate Mureșan's 2026-2028 governing programme (Sep 2026, financialintelligence.ro); Forbes.ro (19 Oct 2026) reports it in the programme of the Niculescu cabinet invested on 20 Oct 2026, while the programme approved by Hotărârea Parlamentului nr. 4/2026 (M.Of. 876 of 20 Oct 2026) carried no such item when checked on 2026-10-21 - sources disagree, and either way it needs a law or OUG in the M.Of. REJECTED BY THE SENATE: bill L546/2026 (AUR deputies' proposal to complete Codul fiscal art. 291; permanent 9% on delivery of a first home) drew a negative Government opinion (28 Sep 2026) and a negative budget-committee report (22 Oct 2026), and the Senate, first chamber, rejected it on 26 Oct 2026 (29 for, 24 against, 51 abstentions) and sent it to the Chamber of Deputies, the decision chamber - a proposal, not law | MF notice 21 Sep 2026 https://mfinante.gov.ro/static/10/Mfp/infotva/comunicatTVA9_21092026.pdf + ANAF AJFP Argeș AGG_DEX-17243/18.08.2026 https://static.anaf.ro/static/3/Ploiesti/20260818161428_comunicat%20ajfp%20arges%20-%20tva%20locuinte%20site.pdf + Legea 161/2026 (lege5 copy) https://lege5.ro/Gratuit/ge4tamrvha4do/legea-nr-161-2026-privind-unele-masuri-fiscal-bugetare + economedia 1 Oct 2026 https://economedia.ro/tva-de-21-la-locuintele-noi-de-la-1-octombrie.html + legislatie.just.ro Legea 161/2026 https://legislatie.just.ro/Public/DetaliiDocument/313202 + Senate L546/2026 record https://www.senat.ro/legis/lista.aspx?nr_cls=L546&an_cls=2026 + gov.ro acts adopted 20 Oct 2026 https://gov.ro/ro/guvernul/sedinte-guvern/informatie-de-presa-privind-actele-normative-adoptate-in-edinta-guvernului-romaniei-din-20-octombrie-2026 + gov.ro acts adopted 23 Oct 2026 (extraordinary meeting) https://gov.ro/ro/guvernul/sedinte-guvern/informatie-de-presa-privind-actele-normative-adoptate-in-edinta-extraordinara-a-guvernului-romaniei-din-23-octombrie-2026 + Forbes.ro 19 Oct 2026 https://www.forbes.ro/programul-de-guvernare-prelungeste-cu-12-luni-tva-ul-redus-de-9-pentru-locuinte-526833 | 2026-10-28 | 2026-11-15 | 2 | --tax --notary`

- `ongoing | ownership | CORRECTED — 'non-EU citizens cannot own ANY land' overstated the rule. Constitution Art. 22(1) (amended by ДВ бр. 18 of 25 Feb 2005, in force since 1 Jan 2007, not amended since) with Property Act Art. 29(1): foreigners and foreign legal persons acquire land only on the terms that follow from EU accession, under an international treaty ratified by a two-thirds majority (Art. 22(2)), promulgated and in force, or, for natural persons, by inheritance by law. The EU route (Property Act Art. 29(2)-(3), ЗСПЗЗ Art. 3(5)-(6)) covers EU and EEA nationals and legal persons, so the restriction applies to non-EU/EEA buyers: they may freely acquire buildings and limited real rights (Property Act Art. 29(4)) but not land, except by treaty or inheritance by law; the usual route for non-agricultural land is a Bulgarian OOD/EOOD, but a company whose partners or shareholders are such foreigners cannot acquire or hold agricultural land (ЗСПЗЗ Art. 3(7)(2)); a non-qualifying heir must transfer inherited agricultural land within 3 years (ЗСПЗЗ Art. 3b). EU/EEA buyers are not fully unrestricted after 2014: ЗСПЗЗ Art. 3c (ДВ бр. 38/2014) requires more than 5 years of residence or establishment in Bulgaria to buy agricultural land; CJEU C-562/22 (18 Jan 2024) held that requirement incompatible with Art. 63 TFEU (secondary — apis.bg, lex.bg; not confirmed on curia.europa.eu) — verify how Art. 3c is applied with a Bulgarian notary before an agricultural-land purchase | State Gazette No. 18 of 25 Feb 2005 (constitutional amendment) — https://dv.parliament.bg/DVWeb/showMaterialDV.jsp?idMat=14898 + Property Act (Закон за собствеността) Art. 29 + ЗСПЗЗ Art. 3-3c | 2026-10-21 | 2027-01-19 | 1 | --tax (ownership)`

Four private-member proposals: a cross-party DIKO/DISY/DIPA bill (a third-country national limited to one home or apartment on a single plot; at least 51% Cyprus/EU/EEA control of acquiring legal entities; forest and agricultural land banned), two AKEL bills (one home up to 200 m², one shop up to 200 m² or one office up to 300 m² exempt from Cabinet approval; forest and agricultural land and purchases near the ceasefire line or critical infrastructure banned) and a DISY (Georgiou) bill on how applications are filed. A committee/Interior Ministry text built from two of them was pulled from the April 2026 plenum at the last minute; the House Interior Committee reopened the bills on 3 Sep 2026, where the Interior Minister said the government would fold them into an updated law (working draft bans agricultural land and land next to the ceasefire line or critical infrastructure and caps plot and home size; home size not settled); purchases by companies were scheduled for an October committee session. A Cyprus Mail editorial of 27 Oct 2026 says the discussion "resumed at the House interior committee last week" and that the committee "will discuss the matter again next week" and "expects the government to submit a comprehensive bill"; no government bill or plenum vote reported. News-tier only (no bill text or committee record read; parliament.cy returned a Cloudflare 403 on 2026-10-28); if enacted this becomes Tier 1 for --tax (ownership) and --visa | Cyprus Property Buyers news (secondary) — https://news.cyprus-property-buyers.com/2026/09/02/parliament-reopens-debate-on-foreign-property-ownership-in-cyprus/id=00173129 + House of Representatives — https://www.parliament.cy/easyconsole.cfm/id/348 + Cyprus Mail editorial 27 Oct 2026 (secondary) — https://cyprus-mail.com/2026/10/27/our-view-strict-rules-on-property-sales-to-third-country-nationals-long-overdue | 2026-10-28 | 2026-11-20 | 4 | --tax (ownership) --visa`

- `ongoing | rental | Queenstown Lakes District STVA — CORRECTION, the flat "25% rates uplift" is wrong: QLDC publishes TWO bands — "Operating under 180 days means your property will be rated as Mixed Use increasing your rates payments by 25-35%" and "Operating between 181 to 365 days means your property will be rated as Accommodation increasing your rates payments by 50-80%"; a Homestay is Mixed Use at 25-35%; rating changes take effect only from the next rating year "beginning 1st July". De-minimis is CUMULATIVE AVAILABILITY, not one block — rates are unaffected "unless you only make your property available for short-term visitors for up to 28 days", and "These 28 days can include multiple stays by different guests and refers to the total length of time the property is available to be rented" (the live page governs over any older QLDC factsheet wording). STVA means paying guests staying under 90 days; the same guests staying 90 days or more are tenants. The 90-night cap is ZONE-SPECIFIC, not district-wide: verified verbatim for Lower Density Suburban Residential std 7.5.18.1 "The total nights of occupation by paying guests on a site do not exceed a cumulative total of 90 nights per annum from the date of initial registration" and the same 90 nights in Medium Density Residential std 8.5.17 (PDP Decisions Version Jun 2023); in both chapters Residential Visitor Accommodation is Permitted (7.4.6 / 8.4.7A) and breaching one of its standards is Restricted Discretionary (RD) per the Non-compliance status column. QLDC publishes a separate standards sheet per zone and states the rules "will depend on which District Plan Zone your property lies within"; a parcel showing no zone on the PDP map is still under the Operative District Plan (ask the QLDC Duty Planner) — never assume 90 nights or RD without checking the parcel's zone on QLDC ePlan. Homestays (resident on site): in most zones no night limit but at most five paying guests per night; never permitted in the General Industrial & Service Zone. - `2027-01-01 (CBS reference rate pending) | tax | LC 214/2025 property regime from 2027 — CBS go-live, rental rate and the individual-taxpayer test: for taxable events 1 Jan 2027 to 31 Dec 2028 the CBS rate is the art. 14 rate (absent a specific federal law, the CBS reference rate fixed by the Senate, art. 14 §3) minus 0.1 percentage point, the cut applied proportionally in reduced-rate regimes (art. 347 and §1 I); IBS only at 0.05% state + 0.05% municipal in 2027-2028, also reduced for reduced-rate regimes (art. 344), full IBS phase-in from 2029. Property operations carry a 50% rate reduction and lettings (locação, cessão onerosa, arrendamento) a 70% reduction (art. 261), so the 2027-2028 CBS on a qualifying letting is 30% x (reference rate − 0.1 pp) (computed); residential lettings by regular-regime taxpayers deduct a redutor social of R$600 per month per property from the base (art. 260 as reworded by LC 227/2026, IPCA-indexed). An individual becomes a regular IBS/CBS taxpayer on property operations when, in the previous calendar year: (I) letting receipts exceeded R$240,000 AND covered more than 3 distinct properties, both required; (II) they sold or assigned rights over more than 3 distinct properties held for less than 5 years (for property received by meação, gift or inheritance the 5 years run from the earlier owner's acquisition, §§3-4); or (III) they sold more than 1 property they built in the 5 years before the sale (art. 251 §1); and within the current year once sales pass the limb II or III limits or letting receipts exceed the R$240,000 limit by 20% (art. 251 §2). The R$240,000 is IPCA-indexed monthly from the law's publication in Jan 2025 (art. 251 §5) — current indexed figure not verified; residential lets of 90 days or less follow the hotel rules and count toward the rental limits (art. 253 §§1-2, added by LC 227/2026). The 2027 CBS reference rate is NOT yet fixed: on 28 Oct 2026 the TCU plenary deliberated its calculation (TC 006.529/2026-7) at 11.3468%, rounded to 11.3% (art. 349 §14), with a 44.16% public-procurement redutor; statutory 2026 deadlines (art. 349 §1 I-II and §5 II, each extended 45 days by art. 353 §2) are 14 Sep 2026 for the Executive proposal to the TCU, 30 Oct 2026 for the TCU calculation to the Senate and 15 Dec 2026 for the Senate resolution; if the Senate has not fixed it by 22 Dec 2026 the TCU-calculated rate applies until it does, a later Senate rate applying from the start of the second month after the month it is fixed and subject to CF art. 150 III b (art. 349 §2 I-II). If 11.3% stands, the 2027 CBS is 11.2% and on a qualifying letting 30% x 11.2% = 3.36% before the redutor social (computed; the 2028 reference rate is calculated and fixed separately, art. 349 I and art. 354). The TCU flags three parameters still undefined on 28 Oct (Imposto Seletivo rates, the residual-IPI product list and ZFM presumed credits, rural-producer and freight presumed-credit percentages); press puts the Fazenda's earlier estimate at 9.21% (Poder360). Resolução CGIBS 14 of 29 Jul 2026 (DOU 31 Jul 2026, per Migalhas, secondary) used a 27.91% combined figure (IBS 18.70%) for methodological and budgetary purposes only. The 26.5% figure is not an automatic ceiling: if the five-year evaluation estimates 2033 reference rates summing above it, the Executive must send Congress a bill to bring the sum down (art. 475 §§10-11). The statute disapplies the CF art. 150 III c 90-day rule (art. 349 §1 II); a ConJur opinion of 15 Oct 2026 argues CF art. 195 §6 still applies to the CBS — disputed. Quote any 2027 rental rate only as TCU-calculated and pending the Senate resolution; re-check 2026-12-16 for it | Planalto LC 214/2025 https://www.planalto.gov.br/ccivil_03/leis/lcp/lcp214.htm + Migalhas 25 Sep 2026 (secondary) https://www.migalhas.com.br/depeso/465114/reforma-tributaria--ibs-e-cbs-em-2027-aliquotas-e-proximos-passos + ConJur 15 Oct 2026 (secondary) https://conjur.com.br/2026-out-15/aliquota-da-cbs-para-2027-e-anterioridade-nonagesimal-uma-controversia-constitucional-em-formacao + TCU CBS 2027 https://sites.tcu.gov.br/reforma-tributaria/visao-cbs-2027.html + Poder360 28 Oct 2026 (press) https://www.poder360.com.br/poder-economia/tcu-fixa-aliquota-da-cbs-em-113-para-2027/ | 2026-10-28 | 2026-12-16 | 3 | --tax --rental`

If approved, from 1 Jan 2027: homestead exemption for non-school levies $150,000, rising to $250,000 on 1 Jan 2028 and CPI-indexed after that (the $25,000 school-levy exemption is unchanged); owners who were not Florida residents on 31 Dec 2026 get the existing exemption, with the increased amount from the fifth year of exemption; annual assessment-increase cap on non-homestead property (second homes, rentals, commercial) cut from 10% to 5% for non-school levies, still resetting to just value on a change of ownership; the Legislature must set a procedure for counties and municipalities to raise the homestead exemption up to full assessed value, and special districts may do the same subject to referendum approval; county and municipal property-tax revenue restricted to listed core purposes, though per the ballot summary other expenditures may be approved by county officers or county or municipal governing bodies unless prohibited by general law. Foreign and non-resident owners cannot claim homestead, so the 5% non-homestead cap is the part that reaches them. - `2027-01-07 | ownership | 21st Century ROAD to Housing Act (H.R. 6644) became Public Law 119-101 without the President's signature on 11 Jul 2026 (House passed it 358-32 on 23 Jun 2026). Section 1001 'Homes are for people, not corporations' (42 USC 19521): from 7 Jan 2027 (180 days after enactment) until its repeal 15 years later (7 Jan 2042) a large institutional investor may not purchase, or contract to purchase, a single-family home (structure with 2 or fewer dwelling units; manufactured homes excluded). Large institutional investor = for-profit entity in the single-family-home business that, alone or in concert, controls at least 350 such homes in aggregate (pre-enactment holdings count; homes bought after enactment in an excepted purchase do not; government entities excluded). Excepted purchases include homes built, renovated or converted for sale, build-to-rent, renovate-to-rent with improvements of at least 15% of the price, qualifying rent-to-own and homeownership programs, foreclosure and loss mitigation, purchases from another large investor, 55+ communities and restructurings of pre-enactment holdings. No investor must sell homes bought before enactment. Civil penalty up to $1,000,000 per violation or 3x the purchase price, whichever is greater (Treasury, or the Attorney General at Treasury's request). Each large investor notifies HUD of its home count by city and state by 7 Jan 2027 and by 31 Dec each year after; HUD renter outreach resource (toll-free line + website) due 7 Jan 2027. Treasury published its proposed implementing rule (new 31 CFR part 36, RIN 1505-AC96, docket TREAS-DO-2026-0529) in the Federal Register on 23 Oct 2026 (91 FR 66799) — PROPOSED, not adopted; comments due 23 Nov 2026. It is a first phase: it would define 'passive investor' and when an entity is in the single-family-home business, set terms for the build-to-rent (homes never owner-occupied, bought within 18 months of the certificate of occupancy, first let to renters), renovate-to-rent (homes failing structural or core-system code elements, improvements of at least 15% of the price net of the tax-assessed land value, 10% structural/core-system safe harbor), homeownership and boost-homeownership programs, and count one purchase by several large investors as a single violation for the penalty cap; Treasury expects to consider later rulemakings on other excepted-purchase categories, compliance procedures, reporting and recordkeeping, and enforcement actions, and HUD will prescribe the annual notice form. Individual buyers, foreign or domestic, are not restricted; the buyer effect is indirect (less institutional competition at purchase, a smaller buyer pool for 1-2 unit homes at resale) | congress.gov P.L. 119-101 enrolled text `https://www.congress.gov/119/plaws/publ101/PLAW-119publ101.pdf` + House Financial Services Committee release 11 Jul 2026 + Treasury NPRM, Federal Register 91 FR 66799, 23 Oct 2026 `https://www.govinfo.gov/content/pkg/FR-2026-10-23/pdf/2026-21675.pdf` + CRS R49354 (everycrsreport.com mirror, secondary) | 2026-10-28 | 2026-11-24 | 3 | --price --exit`

- `2018-06 | rental | 民泊法 Minpaku law 180-night/yr cap unchanged and in force — 住宅宿泊事業法 art.2(3) 一年間で百八十日 per the e-Gov current consolidated text; amended only by two cross-statute 関係法律 整備/整理 laws — the e-Gov revision history lists 令和元年法律第37号 (成年被後見人等 整備法, 2019) and 令和4年法律第68号 (the 刑法 整理法, latest, in force 1 Jun 2025) and no scheduled revision (checked 2026-10-28), and the MLIT Minister (press conference 29 Sep 2026) put the priority on supervision under the current Act. art.18 lets 都道府県, or a 保健所設置市等 (a 保健所設置市 or 特別区 running the Act's administration under art.68(1)), restrict operating periods by 条例, 区域を定めて; the 15 Jul 2026 national technical advice says such a 条例 may set zero days (ゼロ日規制) within reasonably necessary limits, so 180 nights is a ceiling (see the 2026-07-15 entry). Kyoto City escalating: under the 2018 条例 only host-absent (家主不在型) minpaku in 住居専用地域 are confined to the 15 Jan noon–16 Mar noon window — host-resident (家主居住型) operations and certified 京町家 businesses (認定京町家事業: no more than 9 guests in one group, a face-to-face explanation of the 京町家 and a 現地対応管理者 within a range the mayor approves) may operate up to the national 180 days (Kyoto City FAQ A1-6 and 認定京町家事業 guide); per the 2026-01-29 mayoral briefing late 2-monthly reports draw immediate 公表 / 過料 / 業務停止命令 / 廃止命令 from Feb 2026 and night / early-morning spot inspections expand from Apr 2026. The city's 本会議 answers (京都市会だより No.149, 2026-04-15) keep the target of a 条例改正 proposal within FY2026. The city's own 素案 (地域の実情を踏まえた民泊規制強化策（素案）, ［資料2］ of the 2nd 京都市にふさわしい民泊の在り方検討会議, 31 Aug 2026) proposes a zero-day rule (いわゆるゼロ日規制) for 届出住宅 in 第1種・第2種 低層・中高層 住居専用地域 and in 工業地域, host-present or host-absent — existing facilities exempt, so in practice new filings; 京町家 in principle covered (under consideration); the industrial-zone limb still flagged as needing justification — and continuous staff presence (常駐) on site or in an adjacent building for every 届出住宅 and 簡易宿所 (applying it to existing facilities after a grace period under consideration). City schedule: 3rd meeting 2 Nov 2026, regulation draft during Nov followed by about one month of public comment, final draft Jan 2027, amendment bill to the Feb 2027 市会 — proposed, NOT enacted; the city's 26 Oct 2026 notice confirms the 3rd meeting for 2 Nov 2026 with 地域の実情を踏まえた民泊規制案 on the agenda. Separately, 14 共産 group members filed member bill 市会議第9号 on 17 Sep 2026: minpaku barred all year (4月1日正午から翌年4月1日正午まで) in the four 住居専用地域, on plots reached only by a flag-shaped strip, a dead-end road or a road under 4 m wide, within 110 m of the facilities listed in 旅館業法 art.3(3), and in a 宿泊施設規制特別用途地区 (companion bill 市会議第10号), plus a neighbours' no-objection document at filing; in force 1 Apr 2027, existing filings exempt from the new period limits and on the old rules to 31 Mar 2030; no result on the council's 9月市会 page (updated 30 Sep 2026; closing 本会議 30 Oct 2026) — member bill, NOT enacted. Osaka 特区民泊 closure and Osaka City 条例: see the 2026-05-29 entry; Tokyo ward overlays: see the 2026-07-01 (Shibuya and neighbouring wards) and 2026-12-16 (23-ward wave) entries. | 観光庁 民泊ポータル + `https://www.city.kyoto.lg.jp/hokenfukushi/cmsfiles/contents/0000351/351332/04_2kisyakaiken.pdf` + `https://www2.city.kyoto.lg.jp/shikai/img/joho/dayori/dayori1490203.pdf` + Kyoto 素案 `https://www.city.kyoto.lg.jp/templates/shingikai_kekka/cmsfiles/contents/0000357/357913/03-1_kyoukasaku.pdf` + Kyoto 第3回 notice `https://www.city.kyoto.lg.jp/sankan/page/0000358889.html` + 市会議第9号 `https://www2.city.kyoto.lg.jp/shikai/img/honkaigi/gian/R8-9/shikaigi08-09.pdf` + Kyoto FAQ `https://www.city.kyoto.lg.jp/hokenfukushi/cmsfiles/contents/0000287/287839/12FAQ.pdf` + MLIT 大臣会見 2026-09-29 `https://www.mlit.go.jp/report/interview/daijin260929.html` + e-Gov 429AC0000000065 | 2026-10-28 | 2026-11-03 | 2 | --rental`
- `2026-05-29 | rental | Osaka 特区民泊 (国家戦略特区 外国人滞在施設経営事業) new certification ENDED — Osaka City stopped accepting new certification applications, and change applications that add rooms or increase floor area, on 29 May 2026 (令和8年5月29日); the city calls this an end (終了), not a pause. It follows a change to the 関西圏国家戦略特別区域計画 certified by the Prime Minister on 28 Nov 2025 (city decision 30 Sep 2025); applications filed by 29 May and certified afterwards count as pre-cutoff, and facilities certified by then may keep operating as before; no resumption announced as of 2026-10-21. The city's designation covered the whole of Osaka City (大阪市域全域, designated 1 May 2014), not only selected wards; minimum stay 2 nights / 3 days. Osaka Prefecture's own jurisdiction: new certification also ended 29 May 2026 in 29 municipalities and in Kawachinagano's 第1種住居地域; Yao and Neyagawa had closed on 28 Nov 2025, and Sakai, Toyonaka, Suita, Takatsuki, Hirakata, Higashiosaka and Katano do not run the scheme; the prefecture lists Izumisano, Kaizuka, Habikino and Kawachinagano (outside its 第1種住居地域) as the remaining areas. 簡易宿所 fallback tightened: Osaka City's amended 旅館業法の施行等に関する条例 (passed 30 Sep 2026, promulgated 2 Oct 2026, takes effect 30 Oct 2026) requires RC or SRC construction, or soundproofing or hotel use the mayor accepts as equivalent, for new permits or floor-area increases in buildings that have residents, staff on site while guests use the facility, and neighbour notification for facilities under 200 m²; facilities permitted, or with an application or 計画届 filed, by the effective date keep the old construction rule (until a later floor-area increase) and the old notification rule, but the staff-presence duty has no transitional measure and applies to every facility from 30 Oct 2026. Osaka City 住宅宿泊事業 条例: barred in all periods in 第1種・第2種低層 and 第1種・第2種中高層住居専用地域 (except lots fronting a road at least 4 m wide) and Monday noon–Friday noon within 100 m of elementary and compulsory-education schools; not applied where none of the cases in 法第11条第1項 apply (host present, no more than five guest rooms) | Osaka City `https://www.city.osaka.lg.jp/kenko/page/0000382418.html` + `https://www.city.osaka.lg.jp/kenko/page/0000341012.html` + written reply `https://www.city.osaka.lg.jp/templates/dantaikyogi/cmsfiles/contents/0000670/670409/10123456.pdf` + 住宅宿泊事業 `https://www.city.osaka.lg.jp/kenko/page/0000422269.html` + 旅館業条例 改正案内 `https://www.city.osaka.lg.jp/kenko/cmsfiles/contents/0000435/435704/kouhuannai3.pdf` + Osaka Prefecture `https://www.pref.osaka.lg.jp/o100090/kankyoeisei/minpaku/index.html` | 2026-10-21 | 2027-01-19 | 2 | --rental`
- `2026-07-15 | rental | ゼロ日規制 technical advice (技術的助言) issued jointly by MHLW, MLIT and 観光庁 (健生衛発0715第1号・国不動第129号・観観産第78号, under 地方自治法 art.245-4(1); a technical advice is not legally binding), addressed to 都道府県, 保健所設置市 and 特別区: a 住宅宿泊事業法 art.18 条例 may impose a zero-day rule (ゼロ日規制) within reasonably necessary limits — e.g. where minpaku is expected to grow, ban NEW 住宅宿泊事業 in defined residential or school-vicinity areas or limit it to 土日祝前日等; where there is strong need and no other adequate remedy, EXISTING 届出住宅 may also be restricted after a grace period. A 条例 may also require ICT management (noise meters, entrance cameras, about one year of data kept) and set other reasonable limits such as check-in/out hours and guest caps separately from art.18. Supersedes guideline (施行要領) 2-4.(1)②, which had called zero-day rules 「適切ではない」; the guideline itself not yet revised per the 観光庁 portal on 30 Sep 2026 (revisit earlier once the revised 施行要領 is published). The Act is NOT amended — 180-night cap (art.2(3)) unchanged; the MLIT Minister (29 Sep 2026) put the priority on full use of the supervisory powers in the current Act and announced no move to licensing. In a zero-day area a change of operator requires a new art.3 filing, so the business cannot continue in that dwelling. Net effect: 180 nights is a ceiling that a local 条例 can cut to zero for new filings | 観光庁 報道発表 2026-07-15 `https://www.mlit.go.jp/kankocho/news06_00067.html` + notice text `https://www.mlit.go.jp/kankocho/content/002011734.pdf` + MLIT 大臣会見 2026-09-29 `https://www.mlit.go.jp/report/interview/daijin260929.html` + 松田法律事務所 legal note (secondary) `https://jmatsuda-law.com/legal-note/2026-18-1` | 2026-10-21 | 2027-01-19 | 2 | --rental`

- `2026-03-01 | rental | Kyoto lodging tax restructure IN FORCE since 1 Mar 2026 (総務大臣 consent 3 Oct 2025) — five bands per person per night on the room-only charge (meals and consumption tax excluded, service charges included): ¥200 under ¥6,000 (据置き, new band) / ¥400 ¥6,000–20,000 / ¥1,000 ¥20,000–50,000 / ¥4,000 ¥50,000–100,000 / ¥10,000 ¥100,000+ (new top band); applies to every lodging facility in the city and is fixed by the night of stay, so earlier bookings owe the new amount for nights from 1 Mar 2026. - `2026-07-01 | rental | Tokyo Shibuya-ku minpaku 条例 (渋谷区住宅宿泊事業の適正な運営に関する条例) amendment (公布 26 Mar 2026; the art.6 prior-notice change took effect on 公布) applies to 届出住宅 filed on or after 1 Jul 2026. Calendar unchanged by the amendment and seasonal, never a weekends-only rule: in restricted zones minpaku is barred 5 Apr–20 Jul, 29 Aug to the Wednesday of the week before the 2nd Monday of October, the Saturday of that week to 25 Dec, and 7 Jan–25 Mar, leaving 63 days a year (ward figure). New rules: restricted zones = 第1種・第2種低層住居専用地域, 第1種・第2種中高層住居専用地域 and 第1種・第2種文教地区 plus newly added 第1種・第2種住居地域 and 準住居地域; the 180-day exception needs an individual operator with no more than 5 rooms who is present during stays or whose own residence is in the same building, on the same plot or adjacent; prior notice to neighbours moves from 7 to 60 days before filing and must be reported to the ward. Filings made by 30 Jun 2026 keep the old zones and the old exception (residence or management-company office within about 100 m, 町会 membership, face-to-face notice, a complaint-response set-up and an annual 特例届出). Neighbouring ward rules: Shinjuku-ku bars minpaku Monday noon–Friday noon in all four 住居専用地域 types (ward page updated 2026-03-10; residential-zone ban proposed, see the 2026-12-16 entry); Minato-ku restricts only host-absent minpaku, in the four 住居専用地域 types plus 文教地区, barred 11 Jan noon–20 Mar noon, 11 Apr noon–10 Jul noon and 1 Sep noon–20 Dec noon (ward page updated 2025-12-26); Nakano-ku bars Monday noon–Friday noon in 住居専用地域 with a host-cohabiting (家主同居型) exception by ward permit (ward page updated 2026-10-22); an amendment ENACTED by the ward assembly on 16 Oct 2026 (第74号議案, filed 24 Sep 2026) takes effect 1 Apr 2027: 第1種・第2種住居地域 and 準工業地域 join the restricted zones (about 70% → about 80% of the ward, ward figure) and the barred period becomes the whole week (日曜日から土曜日まで, holiday carve-out deleted), so a filing made there from 1 Apr 2027 can operate only with the host-cohabiting permit; filings made before 1 Apr 2027 keep the old zones and period, but from that date every operator must submit a waste-contractor document and breaches of orders can be published by name; the parallel 旅館業法施行条例 amendment (第73号議案, same dates) requires staff on site during operating hours, except for operators already running before 1 Apr 2027 | 渋谷区 条例・規則（令和8年3月26日公布）改正の概要 `https://files.city.shibuya.tokyo.jp/assets/12995aba8b194961be709ba879857f70/d06ba0466e004a5ca37f230da4c46e12/minpaku_kaisei_gaiyo.pdf` + Shinjuku-ku, Minato-ku and Nakano-ku ward minpaku pages + 中野区議会 議案一覧 `https://kugikai-nakano.jp/honkaigi.html` (第74号議案 `https://kugikai-nakano.jp/gian/2693018650.pdf`, 第73号議案 `https://kugikai-nakano.jp/gian/2693018640.pdf`) + 中野区長記者会見 2026-10-22 `https://www.city.tokyo-nakano.lg.jp/kusei/public/houdou/2026/20261021kisyakaiken.files/261022kaikennhonntai.pdf` | 2026-10-28 | 2027-04-02 | 3 | --rental`
- `2026-12-16 (Toshima; Kita 2027-01-07; Bunkyo Jan 2027; Shinjuku proposed 2027-06-01) | rental | Tokyo 23-ward minpaku 条例 wave after the 15 Jul 2026 national zero-day advice — with Kita-ku's first overlay 条例 (passed per Asahi and promulgated per the ward site, 7 Oct 2026) all 23 wards have adopted their own minpaku rules, not all in force yet (Asahi 2026-10-07, secondary). Toshima-ku (amendment adopted Dec 2025; procedural rules in force 15 Dec 2025): from 16 Dec 2026 minpaku may operate ward-wide only 15 Mar–10 Apr, 1 Jul–31 Aug and 15 Dec–14 Jan (120 days a year), existing facilities included; new openings barred in 住居専用・住居・準工業 zones and 文教地区 (about 70% of the ward); breaches of the area and period limits carry a 過料 of up to ¥50,000 (ward pages, primary). Kita-ku: from 7 Jan 2027 host-absent (不在型) minpaku can make no new filings in any 住居系 zone (the four 住専 zones, 第一種/第二種住居, 準住居, 田園住居) or in 地区計画 districts that restrict hotels; host-resident and adjacent-resident types not restricted (Kita-ku site — confirm the promulgated text before relying). Shinjuku-ku (PROPOSED, not enacted): the ward's 条例改正案（骨子） is in public comment 15 Oct–13 Nov 2026 (ward page updated 2026-10-15). It would make 住居専用地域, 住居地域, 準工業地域 and 文教地区 restricted zones with no new minpaku (ゼロ日規制 in the residential zones), except a ward-certified 特例住宅 — owned by an operator who speaks Japanese and has lived in it or next door for 3+ years, with separate lockable bedrooms if host-present — allowed 120 days a year: 15 Mar noon–11 Apr noon, 1 Jul noon–1 Sep noon and 15 Dec noon–15 Jan noon; 近隣商業 and 商業 zones would be limited to the same 120-day windows; existing facilities fall under the zone and day limits 2 years after the amendment takes effect, while face-to-face guest ID checks, a Japanese-speaking responder who can reach the property within about 30 minutes (おおむね) and a contracted waste hauler apply to them from in-force; neighbour notice moves from 7 to 30 days before filing, in writing plus a briefing session. Ward timetable: comment results to the 23 Dec 2026 調整会議, bill to the 2027 第1回定例会 after a 10 Feb 2027 committee report, promulgation planned 24 Mar 2027, in force planned 1 Jun 2027, so existing facilities would be covered from 1 Jun 2029 (computed). Bunkyo-ku: per TOKYO MX (secondary), the assembly passed a member-proposed amendment on 7 Oct 2026 adding 商業 and 近隣商業 zones, making the weekday ban (currently Sunday noon to Friday noon in 住専・住居・準工業・文教) ward-wide from Jan 2027 — ward-site confirmation pending. Shinjuku limb re-checked 2026-10-28 on the ward site; Toshima, Kita and Bunkyo limbs as of 2026-10-21 | Asahi 2026-10-07 `https://www.asahi.com/articles/ASVB72545VB7OXIE00TM.html` + Tokyo Shimbun 2026-09-27 `https://www.tokyo-np.co.jp/article/517933` + TOKYO MX 2026-10-07 `https://s.mxtv.jp/mxnews/article/chiiki/1xtx8vnknnjjw9blk.html` (all secondary) + Toshima-ku 条例改正 page `https://www.city.toshima.lg.jp/214/kurashi/ese/kankyoese/minpaku/kaisei.html` + Toshima-ku まちづくり newsletter `https://www.city.toshima.lg.jp/documents/3079/20260318151559.pdf` + Shinjuku-ku public-comment page (updated 2026-10-15) `https://www.city.shinjuku.lg.jp/kenkou/kenko01_002177_01_00001.html` + Shinjuku-ku 改正案（骨子） `https://www.city.shinjuku.lg.jp/content/000463511.pdf` + Shinjuku-ku 政策経営会議資料 2026-10-01 `https://www.city.shinjuku.lg.jp/content/000468128.pdf` | 2026-10-28 | 2026-12-17 | 3 | --rental`

Nationality-recording added to 農地法 §3 (since 2023-09-01) / 国土利用計画法 施行規則 (corporate acquirers, from 2026-04-01) / 不動産登記規則 (every ownership 保存 or 移転 registration, from 2026-10-05 — see the 2026-10-05 entry) is data capture, NOT a foreigner approval gate. | MLIT 土地取引規制制度 https://www.mlit.go.jp/totikensangyo/totikensangyo_tk2_000019.html + 内閣府 重要土地等調査法 https://www.cao.go.jp/tochi-chosa/ + r/japanlife 1oz5mvf https://www.reddit.com/r/japanlife/comments/1oz5mvf/ (anecdote) | 2026-05-15 | 2027-05-15 | 4 | --permits --buying-process --scams --remote`

Norma General 03-2022 repealed only art. 33 of Norma 03-2018. The year-of-change + 10-years window (or instead IRPF at the reduced 7% rate of art. 37 Título 7 TO 2023) came from Ley 19.904 art. 1 (promulgada 18 Sep 2020) for residents from fiscal 2020 (now art. 24 Título 7 TO 2023). A) in each year; not resident in the 2 prior years. - `2024-04-01 | tax | IVA general rate 15% — LRTI Art. 65 (as quoted by SRI) sets a statutory 13% base and lets the President change the general rate by executive decree within a band ("En ningún caso la tarifa podrá ser inferior al 13% ni mayor al 15%"); Decreto Ejecutivo 198 set 15% "Durante el año 2024, desde el 01 de abril de 2024" under the Ley Orgánica para Enfrentar el Conflicto Armado Interno, la Crisis Social y Económica (RO 516), which also set 5% on construction materials from 1 Apr 2024; Decreto Ejecutivo 470 (4th supp. RO 700, 10 Dec 2024) kept 15% for 2025; SRI Circular NAC-DGECCGC25-00000006 (26 Dec 2025) — per RLRTI Art. 141 last para. (added by DE 213, RO 166, 18 Nov 2025) the last decreed rate stays in force, so "la tarifa del IVA que se encuentra vigente es del 15 %" until a new decree; a decree cut can go only to the 13% floor (a return to 12% would need a statutory amendment); holiday-weekend 8% tourism-services decrees (e.g. DE 190, 29–31 Mar 2024) do not change the general rate; NOTE the SRI IVA page body text still reads "vigente es del 0% y del 13%" (re-read 2026-10-21) — contradicted by the SRI circular and by that page's own decree table (which lists DE 198 at 15 % from 1 Apr 2024 but not DE 470 or any 2025-2026 decree); still 15 % as of 2026-10-21: no general-rate decree found in the Presidencia decree register (decrees listed through No. 522 of 15 Oct 2026) or the news searched; the latest tourism holiday decree, DE 513 (signed 29 Sep 2026), set 8 % only on tourism services for 9-11 Oct 2026 and the general rate reverted after | SRI Circular NAC-DGECCGC25-00000006 + SRI IVA page decree table — https://www.sri.gob.ec/o/sri-portlet-biblioteca-alfresco-internet/descargar?id=236482f4-6125-42fd-b073-62c99d08233d&nombre=NAC-DGECCGC25-00000006.pdf | 2026-10-21 | 2027-01-19 | 1 | --tax`
- `2024-12-09 | tax | CORRECTED — Ecuador has NO holding-period test and NO habitual-residence test. LRTI art. 9 num. 14, as substituted by the Ley Orgánica para el Alivio Financiero y el Fortalecimiento Económico de las Generaciones en el Ecuador (5th supp. RO 699, 9 Dec 2024, in force on publication), exempts from income tax "Los generados por la enajenación ocasional de inmuebles de personas jurídicas o naturales. Esta disposición será aplicable hasta por dos (2) enajenaciones por año"; occasional = not part of the seller's ordinary business or habitual activity. RLRTI art. 18 (SRI codification): exempt gains include accessory parking / storage units when transferred in a single act, costs of the exempt sale are not deductible, and sales by businesses doing lotización / urbanización / construcción / promoción of real estate are habitual, not occasional. The same law repealed LODESF Disposición Transitoria Décimo Quinta (exemption for the first transfer of a property for five years after the Nov 2021 law). The superseded 2021 LODESF wording (RO 587 3S, 29 Nov 2021) limited the exemption to natural persons selling housing, its accessories and land. Natural persons running more than two real-estate projects lose the num. 14 exemption for the year in which they obtained an IVA refund (article added by the law in RO 461 S, 20 Dec 2023). A third or later sale in a year, or a habitual seller, is taxable; how the taxable gain is computed and the non-resident rate were not re-read in this pass — data not publicly available here, verify at SRI. Supersedes "0 % after 1-year holding (NOT 5 years)", "UNIQUE LATAM", "LRTI art. 8" and "Ley Orgánica de Reforma Tributaria" (the rule comes from the RO 699 law) | SRI LRTI codification art. 9 num. 14 https://www.sri.gob.ec/o/sri-portlet-biblioteca-alfresco-internet/descargar/12c884d5-c8d9-4171-becc-1513b990b87a/LEY_DE_REGIMEN_TRIBUTARIO_INTERNO_LRTI.pdf + SRI RLRTI codification art. 18 + RO 699 5S text | 2026-10-21 | 2027-01-19 | 1 | --tax --exit`

### 🇦🇲 AM

- `2027-01-01 | tax | Among the Tax Code (HO-165-N) amending laws that take effect 1 Jan 2027, two touch the VAT articles the AM playbook cites (Arts 59 and 64), and neither adds a VAT exemption for selling dwellings. HO-323-N (adopted 3 Jul 2026, published 17 Jul 2026) rewrites Art. 64(2)(47) sub-point ժբ (sub-point l in the official English text): the VAT exemption for a bank or credit organisation selling collateral it took over from a natural person who is not an individual entrepreneur or notary now also covers real estate that belonged to such a person, was encumbered by a reverse mortgage and passed to the bank or credit organisation under that reverse-mortgage contract; it also adds Art. 147(1)(19.1), listing monthly payments received under a reverse-mortgage contract among the deducted (non-taxable) incomes for income tax. HO-83-N (adopted 4 Mar 2026, published 31 Mar 2026) repeals Art. 59(7), which made a non-resident EAEU e-commerce platform operator liable for VAT on goods supplied into Armenia, and strikes the matching exception in Art. 59(6)(1); platform-operator VAT liability moves to a new Art. 70(3). The Art. 59(6) rule that a natural person who is not an individual entrepreneur or notary is not a VAT payer is unchanged. Revisit once ARLIS incorporates both laws: re-read Arts 59, 64 and 147 together with any 2027 budget-cycle Tax Code amendments and confirm no residential-sale VAT exemption was added (the AM playbook --tax VAT block cites Arts 59(6), 62(8), 63(1) and 64) | ARLIS — HO-323-N https://www.arlis.am/hy/acts/228214 + HO-83-N https://www.arlis.am/hy/acts/223255 + consolidated Tax Code https://www.arlis.am/en/acts/230455 (amendment notes under Arts 59, 64 and 147) | 2026-10-23 | 2027-01-15 | 3 | --tax --finance`

- `2025-05-31 | regulatory | Foreign Agents Registration Act (GEOFARA, Law N399-IIმს-XIმპ) — adopted 1 Apr 2025, published 2 Apr 2025, in force 31 May 2025; wilful non-registration or other breach punishable by a fine up to GEL 10,000 or imprisonment up to 5 yr (FARA Art. 8 + Criminal Code Art. 355²(2), inserted by Law №400 of 1 Apr 2025); administering body moved from the Anti-Corruption Bureau to the State Audit Office (Auditor General) from 2 Mar 2026 (Law №1292 of 17 Dec 2025, still the only amending act in the matsne consolidated list on 21 Oct 2026); Constitutional Court claim №1865 (GYLA and others) admitted to the Plenum 24 Jun 2025 and a further claim №1883 (Georgian Academy of the Future and others) filed 1 Aug 2025, no final judgment found in the court records read; ECtHR case Georgian Young Lawyers' Association and Others v. Georgia (no. 2) pending, CoE Commissioner for Human Rights written observations submitted 31 Aug 2026 (coe.int); grants-approval regime tightened 4 Mar 2026 — receiving a foreign grant without the required consent is a crime punishable by up to 6 yr (Criminal Code Art. 319¹, Law №1380); Venice Commission opinion CDL-AD(2026)026 on the 4 Mar 2026 amendments strongly recommends reconsidering criminal sanctions (secondary, civil.ge 13 Oct 2026; coe.int PDF returned 403 this pass); PACE resolution of 24 Jun 2026 called for repeal of FARA and the Law on Grants amendments "fully and unconditionally" (secondary, civil.ge); registration trigger (Art. 1(c)) is acting for a foreign principal, with activities wholly or mainly supervised, controlled or financed by it, in political activities, as PR counsel, publicity agent, information-service employee or political consultant, as fund-raiser or disburser, or representing the principal before any state agency or official; Art. 3 exempts private non-political bona fide trade or commerce (d.a) and a lawyer's legal representation before courts or agencies (g); the Act contains no provision on real-estate acquisition, so it does not restrict private residential purchase by foreigners but can reach NGOs, media, companies and advisers doing that work | matsne.gov.ge/en/document/view/6461578 (FARA consolidated) + matsne.gov.ge/ka/document/view/16426 (Criminal Code Arts. 355², 319¹) + constcourt.ge/ka/judicial-acts?legal=18165 + constcourt.ge/ka/judicial-acts?legal=18319 + coe.int/web/commissioner/third-party-interventions + Venice Commission CDL-AD(2025)034 + civil.ge/archives/742301 + civil.ge/archives/754522 (secondary) | 2026-10-21 | 2027-01-19 | 2 | --tax --rental --scams`
- `2026-03-01 | visa | Real-estate SHORT-TERM residence permit (Law on Legal Status of Aliens and Stateless Persons №2045-IIს Art. 15(j) — not the investment permit) now needs non-agricultural real estate with market value above the GEL equivalent of US$150,000 (US$100,000 before 1 Mar 2026), valued by an accredited certified appraiser, and extends to the owner's spouse and children; permit issued and renewed 1 year at a time (Art. 16(1)(f)) and ends if ownership of that property ends; renewal on the SAME property needs no re-valuation (Art. 16(3)), so holders first permitted at US$100k keep renewing while they keep that property; the D5 immigration visa threshold rose to US$150,000 by the same amending law (Art. 7(3)(d.e)). Separate INVESTMENT residence permit Art. 15(h): US$300,000 investment or real estate above US$300,000 → 5-year permit (Art. 16(1)(e), unchanged); holders of a valid investment residence permit are outside the Law on Labour Migration (Art. 1(4)(f)). Cut-off for buyers still applying at US$100k (reported by secondary sources as purchase completed before 1 Mar 2026) is not stated in the consolidated law text nor in amending Law №863 of 26 Jun 2025 (whose Art. 2(2) only commences Art. 1 items 1-3 from 1 Mar 2026, with no grandfathering clause), and the PSDA residence-permit service page lists only the US$150,000 threshold — PSDA administrative practice for pre-1-Mar-2026 purchases unverified, verify at PSDA / sda.gov.ge | Law of Georgia №2045-IIს (consolidated publication 25 Jun 2026, still the latest on 21 Oct 2026) `https://matsne.gov.ge/ka/document/view/2278806` + Law №863 `https://matsne.gov.ge/ka/document/view/6543112` + Law on Labour Migration `https://matsne.gov.ge/ka/document/view/2806732` + sda.gov.ge | 2026-10-21 | 2027-01-19 | 2 | --visa --tax --rental`
- `2026-03-01 | visa | Labor migration amendments (adopted 26 Jun 2025, Law №862) require a right to labour activity for foreign workers engaged by a local employer and for self-employed foreigners (Law Art. 2(c²): any alien without a permanent residence permit doing paid work, incl. trade, services, partner or independent contractor) — no visa-free-stay carve-out appears in the Law or in Government Resolution №70, so the earlier "beyond the visa-free year" qualifier is not supported; lawful work needs an employment contract (not for the self-employed) + right to labour activity + a work residence permit, D1 visa and/or any other residence permit specified by law (Art. 13³), so a visa-free stay alone does not suffice; labour immigrants whose database registration was active on 1 Mar 2026 must obtain the right to labour activity + residence permit by 1 Jan 2027 (Law Art. 17(2²) + Res. №70 Art. 14(2)), and enforcement against self-employed foreigners already active on 1 Mar 2026 started 1 May 2026 (Res. №70 Art. 14(1) as amended by Res. №85 of 26 Feb 2026); unpermitted work costs GEL 2,000 on the employer per worker AND GEL 2,000 on the worker or self-employed foreigner (Art. 16¹), twice that on repeat, and three times the doubled fine for a further breach after a doubled or tripled fine, i.e. GEL 12,000 (computed: 3 x 2 x 2,000); employer procedure under the Res. №70 Rules as amended by Res. №321 of 9 Jul 2026 (in force on publication 10 Jul 2026): the vacancy-posting step was replaced by an employer annual quota — an employer planning more than 5 foreign nationals in a calendar year, or whose foreigners exceed 5% of headcount (unless 5% is fewer than 5 people), must agree a quota with the Agency at least 10 working days before applying, with Revenue Service proof of annual turnover of at least GEL 50,000 per foreigner (GEL 35,000 for educational or medical institutions); exempt from that procedure (Rules Art. 5(1)(ზ)): International Company status (Tax Code Art. 23) and innovative-startup status companies, NCEQE expert-corps international experts, or a vacancy that simultaneously pays over GEL 15,000 gross per month AND requires a relevant higher-education degree (the Agency may still apply the procedure, Art. 5(2)); Annex 3 annual sector quotas: courier 0, passenger transport 0, tourist guides 0, mountain/alpine/ski guides 200; Law №1509 of 15 Apr 2026 (in force on publication) took OUT of scope work done fully remotely for a local employer that needs no entry into Georgia (Art. 1(4)(j)) and work or services for a non-resident tied to that non-resident's activity outside Georgia (Art. 1(4)(k)), but the Res. №70 Rules (Art. 1(3)(ლ) as amended by Res. №321) withhold the non-resident exemption from an individual entrepreneur who needs a residence permit to stay lawfully, a carve-out not in the Law text; also out of scope: short-term professional activity (Rules: max 4 months in aggregate per calendar year, employer pre-registration), public-institution work, category 1-3 directorships (Rules add public-interest entities), heritage-restoration work; holders of a valid investment residence permit are also out of scope (Art. 1(4)(f)); Law №1815 of 25 Jun 2026 (published 29 Jun 2026) added the exemption for holders of the Art. 60 temporary-stay right (aliens who cannot be removed) as Art. 1(4)(ო) — (n) in the official English translation — in force from 1 Sep 2026 (Law №1815 Art. 2; - `2026-07-01 | finance | NBG de-dollarization ceiling on unhedged foreign-currency loans rose to GEL 1,000,000 effective 1 Jul 2026 (in force: the FSR 2026 macroprudential table lists the FX-loan restriction as "1 000 000 ლარის ქვემოთ" dated 01.07.2026, checked 2026-10-27), after GEL 500,000 to GEL 750,000 effective 1 Aug 2025 — FSC decisions read verbatim "the limit on unhedged foreign currency loans will increase from 500,000 GEL to 750,000 GEL, effective from August 1, 2025" and "the limit on unhedged foreign currency loans increases from 750,000 GEL to 1,000,000 GEL, effective from July 1, 2026"; below that ceiling the loan is issued in lari unless the borrower's income is in the loan currency (licensed-lender restatement, Terabank: "A loan or bank credit for amounts less than 1 000 000 GEL can only be issued in the national currency, except when the borrower's income and the loan are in the same currency"). CORRECTION to the prior entry — the FX-mortgage LTV cap was NOT tightened and NBG keys these rules off loan currency versus income currency, never buyer residency: FSR 2025 reads "since 2019, the National Bank of Georgia determined a maximum LTV ratio of 70 percent for mortgage loans issued in foreign currency", and NBG's live PTI and LTV table still reads LTV 90% for GEL loans and 70% for foreign-currency loans (checked 2026-10-27). The only LTV move in the window was a LOOSENING that HELPED buyers with income from abroad — Feb 2025 FSC "temporarily increases the maximum loan-to-value (LTV) ratio by 5 percentage points to 90% for local currency loans secured by real estate, issued to natural persons. Additionally, for individuals receiving income from abroad, the LTV ratio for mortgage loans will be raised by 10 percentage points to 80%". The FSR 2025 vs live-page conflict is RESOLVED: FSR 2025's macroprudential table stamped the foreign-currency LTV row at 80% dated 26.02.2025, but the FSR 2026 table (Georgian, announced in the 30 Sep 2026 FSC release) reads "უცხოური ვალუტით გაცემულ სესხებზე 70% 01.01.2019" (GEL row 90%, 26.02.2025), matching the live page — 70% is the FX cap. The 80% income-from-abroad carve-out appears in neither the live page nor the FSR 2026 table, so whether it still applies is UNVERIFIED; confirm with the lender before budgeting a deposit. Rate stance is "moderately tight" (MPC, 21 Oct 2026), not post-EUR-cycle normalization: MPC raised the refinancing rate 0.25pp to 8.25% on 6 May 2026 and held at 8.25% on 17 Jun, 29 Jul, 9 Sep and 21 Oct 2026, against a 3% inflation target (Oct 2026 MPC: headline inflation 5.6% in Sep 2026, about 2pp of it energy, core 3.5%, services 3.7%; 2026 inflation forecast raised 0.2pp to 5.4%; next MPC 16 Dec 2026). The 30 Sep 2026 FSC meeting left the unhedged-FX limit and LTV untouched and recalibrated the PTI income thresholds (see the 2027-02-01 PTI entry); no FSC decision published since (NBG news, checked 2026-10-27); next FSC meeting 25 Nov 2026 per the 30 Sep 2026 release | NBG PTI and LTV Requirements page + NBG Financial Stability Committee decisions + NBG Monetary Policy Committee decisions + NBG Financial Stability Reports 2025 and 2026 + Terabank + https://nbg.gov.ge/en/page/pti-and-ltv-requirements + `https://nbg.gov.ge/en/media/news/financial-stability-and-monetary-policy-committees-decisi-3` + `https://nbg.gov.ge/media/news/pinansuri-stabilurobis-komitetis-gadatsqvetil-28` + `https://nbg.gov.ge/en/media/news/the-national-bank-of-georgia-decided-to-keep-the-monetary-policy-rate-unchanged-at-8-25-3` + `https://nbg.gov.ge/publications/financial-stability-reports` | 2026-10-27 | 2026-11-26 | 2 | --finance --currency --macro`

### 🇮🇩 ID

- `2021-02 | foreign-buyer | PP No. 18/2021 (ditetapkan and diundangkan 2 Feb 2021, LN 2021/28, in force on promulgation per Pasal 104; JDIH Kota Surabaya lists it as Berlaku with no status change recorded) — Hak Pakai (a right of use, not freehold) is the direct land right open to Orang Asing (Pasal 49(2)(e)); term on Tanah Negara / Tanah Hak Pengelolaan is 30 + 20 extension + 30 renewal = up to 80 yr (Pasal 52(1)), but Hak Pakai over Hak Milik is max 30 yr, renewable by a new akta pemberian hak pakai (Pasal 52(3)), which Pasal 53(3) requires to be made by a PPAT. Eligibility is a "dokumen keimigrasian" (Pasal 69(1)), which the Penjelasan defines as "visa, paspor, atau izin tinggal" — KITAS/KITAP is NOT a precondition in the PP text, and implementing Permen ATR/BPN 18/2021 Pasal 185 likewise asks only for a dokumen keimigrasian "yang diterbitkan oleh instansi yang berwenang" (which document a given Kantor Pertanahan accepts in practice — confirm there). Pasal 1 angka 14 defines Orang Asing as a non-citizen "yang keberadaannya memberikan manfaat, melakukan usaha, bekerja, atau berinvestasi di Indonesia", and Pasal 50 obliges a Hak Pakai holder who no longer meets Pasal 49 to release or transfer the right within 1 year, failing which it is extinguished by law. Foreigners may hold Hak Milik atas Satuan Rumah Susun (Pasal 67(1)(c): an Orang Asing "yang mempunyai izin sesuai dengan ketentuan peraturan perundang-undangan"; Permen 18/2021 Pasal 188(1)) in rumah susun on Hak Pakai OR HGB land (Pasal 71(1)(b)), limited by Pasal 71(2) to KEK, free-trade/free-port zones, kawasan industri and "kawasan ekonomi lainnya", which the Penjelasan defines broadly as "kawasan perkotaan dan/atau kawasan pendukung perkotaan, kawasan pariwisata, atau kawasan yang mendukung pembangunan hunian vertikal dan memberikan dampak ekonomi kepada masyarakat". Pasal 72 limits foreign holdings by minimum price, plot area, number of plots/units and residential use; Permen 18/2021 Pasal 186 sets them: a landed house must be in the rumah mewah category, 1 bidang tanah per orang/keluarga and/or at most 2,000 m2 (more plots or area only "dengan izin Menteri" where the holding has a positive economic and social impact), and a condo must be a rumah susun komersial; Pasal 187(2) leaves the price to a Keputusan Menteri (not read — verify at ATR/BPN). JDIH ATR/BPN lists Permen 18/2021 as Berlaku but dicabut sebagian by Permen ATR/BPN 2/2025 (provisions on changing hak milik into HGB or hak pakai, except in an auction); Permen 2/2025 was itself revoked by Permen ATR/BPN 5/2025 (diundangkan 28 Apr 2025), whose Pasal 20 routes a hak milik to HGB change through an auction or release to State land plus a new grant and does not mention hak pakai — whether the Pasal 187(3) change of Hak Milik or HGB into Hak Pakai on a foreign purchase still operates is data not publicly available — verify at the Kantor Pertanahan | PP 18/2021 official text via JDIH Kota Surabaya `https://jdih.surabaya.go.id/uploads/peraturan/3815_pp_18-2021.pdf` + Permen ATR/BPN 18/2021 via JDIH ATR/BPN `https://jdih.atrbpn.go.id/peraturan/detail/1030/peraturan-menteri-agraria-dan-tata-ruang-kepala-badan-pertanahan-nasional-nomor-18-tahun-2021` + Permen ATR/BPN 2/2025 status `https://jdih.atrbpn.go.id/peraturan/detail/1200/peraturan-menteri-agraria-dan-tata-ruang-kepala-badan-pertanahan-nasional-nomor-2-tahun-2025` | 2026-10-21 | 2027-01-19 | 1 | --tax --visa ownership`
- `ongoing | foreign-buyer | Nominee ownership is void by statute, not merely risky — UU 5/1960 (UUPA) Art. 26(2), text as published on the Ministry ATR/BPN JDIH portal, makes any act intended to transfer hak milik to a foreigner directly or indirectly "adalah batal karena hukum dan tanahnya jatuh kepada Negara" (void by operation of law, and the land falls to the State), adding that "semua pembayaran yang telah diterima oleh pemilik tidak dapat dituntut kembali" (payments the owner has received cannot be reclaimed), with Art. 27(a)(4) extinguishing the hak milik outright — so the foreign payer's outlay is not secured by the land it bought. The statute's own elucidation confirms the legitimate route: "hanya warganegara Indonesia saja yang dapat mempunyai hak milik atas tanah … Orang-orang asing dapat mempunyai tanah dengan hak pakai yang luasnya terbatas". Kanwil BPN Provinsi Bali (statement of 7 Jul 2025 by Kakanwil I Made Daging) records ~463 Hak Pakai parcels held in WNA names Bali-wide (regulator's own wording "sekitar 463", on data through Mar 2025), each generally not exceeding 5,000 m2, and states nominee practice "bersifat tersembunyi dan umumnya baru dapat terungkap ketika muncul persoalan hukum" (hidden, generally surfacing only once a legal problem arises) because the paperwork formally sits in an Indonesian citizen's name. SUPERSEDES the prior wording: no Indonesian court is named "Bali Provincial Court" (Bali's general courts are Pengadilan Negeri Denpasar and Pengadilan Tinggi Denpasar), and the asserted 2023-2025 nominee voidings were never verified — the Direktori Putusan at putusan3.mahkamahagung.go.id returns HTTP 403 to automated retrieval (re-confirmed 2026-10-21), so any circulating 2023-2025 nominee case count is unverified and must be checked manually against the docket | Kementerian ATR/BPN JDIH UU 5/1960 `https://jdih.atrbpn.go.id/peraturan/download/32/UU_05_1960.pdf` + Kanwil BPN Provinsi Bali `https://bali.atrbpn.go.id/berita/kepala-kantor-wilayah-bpn-provinsi-bali-klarifikasi-terkait-isu-pulau-di-bali-yang-dikuasai-wna` (page body is JavaScript-rendered; same statement text read on the Kanwil's official Instagram account kanwil.bpn.bali, post of 7 Jul 2025 `https://www.instagram.com/p/DL1Q5gnTXAm`) | 2026-10-21 | 2027-01-19 | 1 | --tax ownership`
- `2023-08-24 | visa | Second Home Visa (Visa Rumah Kedua, Imigrasi index E33) — CORRECTED, both thresholds are set in US dollars and the property route is condo-only: Permenkumham 22/2023 (in force 24 Aug 2023, BN 2023/651) Pasal 56(2) requires a Jaminan Keimigrasian of either (a) a commitment to keep at least US$130,000 "atau setara" in an account in the applicant's own name at a bank milik negara, or (b) a commitment to buy "properti berupa rumah susun atau apartemen di Indonesia senilai paling sedikit US$1.000.000 (satu juta dolar Amerika) atau setara", to be met within 90 days of the ITAS; Pasal 56(3) lets the Direktur Jenderal re-evaluate both values. The live Imigrasi E33 page (read 2026-10-21; legal basis listed as Permenkumham 22/2023 as amended by Permenkumham 11/2024 plus Kepmen Imigrasi dan Pemasyarakatan M.IP-08.GR.01.01/2025) states the same two options, proof reported to the issuing Kantor Imigrasi within 90 days of entry, the guarantee kept at or above the committed amount while the permit is valid, first permit up to 5 yr with total stay capped at 10 yr, PNBP Rp13,000,000 for up to 5 yr. So the property itself CAN be the qualifying asset, but only a rumah susun / apartemen worth at least US$1M — a landed house or villa on Hak Pakai is not named. - `2022-01-05 | tax | UU HKPD Law 1/2022 (diundangkan 5 Jan 2022, LN 2022/4, in force on promulgation per Pasal 193) reformed local taxation — BPHTB is a kabupaten/kota tax (Pasal 4(2)(b)); statutory ceiling 5% (Pasal 47(1)), the actual rate set by each kabupaten/kota Perda (Pasal 47(2)); the non-taxable value (NPOPTKP) is at least IDR 80,000,000 for the taxpayer's first acquisition in that region (Pasal 46(5)) and at least IDR 300,000,000 for inheritance or hibah wasiat within the direct line incl. spouse (Pasal 46(6)), both set by Perda (Pasal 46(8)); Perda made under UU 28/2009 stayed valid for at most 2 years (Pasal 187(b)). - `2025-10-10 | rental | SUPERSEDED CITATION, not a substantive reversal — Permenparekraf 4/2021, which the ID playbook --rental headline rule cited (playbook line 332, grep-verified 2026-08-07; citation moved to Permenpar 6/2025 on 2026-10-28) as the operative sectoral standard, has been revoked. Permenpar 6/2025 (Standar Kegiatan Usaha, Tata Cara Pelaksanaan Pengawasan, dan Sanksi Administratif pada Penyelenggaraan Perizinan Berusaha Berbasis Risiko Sektor Pariwisata) Pasal 22, in BAB VI Ketentuan Penutup, reads verbatim "... Nomor 4 Tahun 2021 tentang Standar Kegiatan Usaha pada Penyelenggaraan Perizinan Berusaha Berbasis Risiko Sektor Pariwisata (Berita Negara Republik Indonesia Tahun 2021 Nomor 283) dan ... Nomor 8 Tahun 2021 tentang Sanksi Administratif Perizinan Berusaha Berbasis Risiko Sektor Pariwisata dan Ekonomi Kreatif (Berita Negara Republik Indonesia Tahun 2021 Nomor 682), dicabut dan dinyatakan tidak berlaku", and Pasal 23 "Peraturan Menteri ini mulai berlaku pada tanggal diundangkan". The salinan carries "Ditetapkan di Jakarta pada tanggal 10 Oktober 2025"; its Diundangkan date did not render legibly in the copy read here, so the in-force date is taken from BPK JDIH, whose CATATAN reads "Peraturan Menteri Pariwisata ini mulai berlaku pada tanggal 10 Oktober 2025" (BN 2025 No. 793); on 2026-10-28 the BPK JDIH page listed Tanggal Pengundangan and Tanggal Berlaku 10 Oktober 2025, Status Berlaku, and the JDIH Kemenpar record gives promulgation 2025-10-10. WHERE THE STANDARD NOW SITS: in the national ministerial Lampiran, which carries "STANDAR KEGIATAN USAHA VILLA TINGKAT RISIKO MENENGAH RENDAH NO. KBLI: (55193) VILLA" and a parallel "NO. KBLI: (55130) PONDOK WISATA" standard, with compliance evidenced by a "Sertifikat Standar Usaha Villa Dengan Tingkat Risiko Menengah Rendah ... yang diberikan oleh Lembaga Sertifikasi Usaha/Lembaga Sertifikasi Produk Bidang Pariwisata", a third-party conformity body ("lembaga penilaian kesesuaian milik pihak ketiga"). This confirms from the primary text the KBLI 55193 villa code the ID block already uses at playbook line 336. It speaks to where the standard is SET, not to how it is enforced locally, about which this entry now makes no claim. TWO DATES, ONE NOW LAPSED: Pasal 5(2) requires the Sertifikat Standar Usaha Pariwisata "paling lambat 1 (satu) tahun terhitung sejak PB terverifikasi", Pasal 5(3) excepting certain "kegiatan usaha dengan tingkat Risiko menengah rendah tertentu"; and Pasal 21 gives operators whose Risk tier changed "jangka waktu paling lama 1 (satu) tahun terhitung sejak diundangkannya Peraturan Menteri ini", a window that LAPSED on 10 Oct 2026 (computed from the 10 Oct 2025 in-force date); no instrument amending or extending Permenpar 6/2025 located as at 2026-10-28 (on 2026-10-21 BPK JDIH listed Permenpar 1 to 6 of 2026 and none amends it; the 2026 Permenpar No. 6 governs RIDPN stakeholder monitoring; on 2026-10-28 the BPK status block for 6/2025 showed only its Mencabut links, and JDIH Kemenpar listed the same Permenpar 1 to 6 of 2026, newest 6/2026 of 20 Aug 2026, and records 4/2021 and 8/2021 as no longer in force), so an operator whose Risk tier changed should already apply the matching Lampiran standard — verify per business on OSS and re-check BPK JDIH and jdih.kemenpar.go.id for any Perubahan atas Permenpar 6/2025. The transitional article immediately preceding Pasal 21 keeps pre-existing OSS-issued PB and PB UMKU that were verified, approved and still valid before promulgation usable. WITHDRAWN — the prior wording of this line, that Bali villa STR enforcement "varies by kabupaten" and that Pondok Wisata licensing is "patchy", is not carried forward: no primary source is cited for it, and per-kabupaten STR enforcement activity and Pondok Wisata licence-issuance counts are data not publicly available — verify per property with the DPMPTSP and the Dinas Pariwisata of the specific kabupaten or kota where it sits, and against the OSS record for the business itself. DELIBERATELY NOT ASSERTED, in either direction: whether the legacy TDUP instrument survives the risk-based regime, whether Permenpar 6/2025 alters the nationality restriction on Pondok Wisata, and how the parent statute UU 10/2009 should now be cited. None was re-derived in this pass, so do not restate any of them on the strength of this entry. RETRIEVAL CAVEAT: on 2026-08-07 direct scripted retrieval failed from here, peraturan.go.id timing out and peraturan.bpk.go.id returning HTTP 403, so both texts were read through a proxy extractor; and that pass ran no check for any later instrument amending Permenpar 6/2025 (the 2026-10-21 BPK JDIH check recorded above located none). RE-READ 2026-10-28: the salinan from JDIH Kemenpar (562 pp.) matches the Pasal 5(2), 21, 22 and 23 quotes and the KBLI 55193 / 55130 Lampiran headings above (its Diundangkan date has the same rendering gap), and the BPK JDIH page was read in a browser | Permenpar 6/2025 salinan on peraturan.go.id + BPK JDIH Permenpar 6/2025 + `https://peraturan.go.id/files/permenparekraf-no-6-tahun-2025.pdf` + `https://peraturan.bpk.go.id/Details/331639/permenpar-no-6-tahun-2025` + JDIH Kemenpar `https://api-jdih.kemenpar.go.id/items/regulations?filter[regulation_year][_eq]=2025&filter[regulation_number][_eq]=6` | 2026-10-28 | 2027-10-28 | 4 | --rental`
- `2025-07-01 | rental | Bali Foreign Tourist Levy IDR 150,000 cashless e-Payment only, amount unchanged since the 14 Feb 2024 launch and reconfirmed in Perda Bali 6/2023 Pasal 5(2) as amended by Perda Bali 2/2025 (ditetapkan + diundangkan Denpasar 20 Jun 2025, in force 1 Jul 2025, Lembaran Daerah 2025 No. 2, status Berlaku on peraturan.go.id). Levy is due on EACH entry to Bali "secara langsung dari luar negeri atau secara tidak langsung melalui wilayah lainnya di Indonesia" (Pasal 4A(1)), but one payment covers that visit: Pasal 5(1) runs "selama Wisatawan Asing berwisata ke Bali dan sebelum meninggalkan wilayah Indonesia" (carried over from old Pasal 5(2), not a 2025 change), and a duplicate paid on the same arrival date is refundable under Pergub 25/2025 Pasal 15A. Payment DEADLINE relaxed, not the pre-payment option — advance payment was always allowed: old Pasal 5(4) required payment "sebelum atau pada saat memasuki pintu kedatangan di Bali", new Pasal 5(3) allows "sebelum atau selama berwisata di Bali", and Pergub Bali 25/2025 Pasal 12(1) lists arrival by air-sea-land, during-stay, OR "keberangkatan Wisatawan Asing keluar Bali" (departure) as payment points. New Pasal 4A(2) EXEMPTS transport crew, KITAS/KITAP holders, diplomatic and service visas, family-reunification visas, student visas, golden visas, and "pemegang jenis visa lainnya" — an open catch-all with detail delegated to a Pergub by Pasal 4A(4), so treat the list as open, not closed; the KITAS/KITAP exemption is the material one for resident foreign owners. Sanctions RESTRUCTURED rather than tightened: old Pasal 6(2) flatly provided that non-payers "dilarang berwisata ke Bali"; new BAB VIIIA Pasal 16A substitutes graduated administrative sanctions (teguran lisan, teguran tertulis, and-or "tidak mendapatkan pelayanan di Daya Tarik Wisata"). Procedure is Pergub Bali 2/2024 ONLY as amended by Pergub Bali 25/2025 (ditetapkan 20 Jun 2025, in force 1 Jul 2025, Berita Daerah 2025 No. 28), which amends Pasal 1 angka 11-12, 4, 9(2), 12, 13, 14(1) and 14(4), 16(1), inserts Pasal 15A and BAB IVA Pasal 22A-22C Imbal Jasa capped "paling tinggi 3%" of levy transactions, and deletes BAB V and Pasal 23 — citing Perda 2/2025 Pasal II(1) alone points at superseded procedure text. No 2026 instrument superseding either found as at 2026-10-21: the Bali JDIH register (dashboard-jdih.baliprov.go.id API, terms pungutan and wisatawan asing) lists no later levy Perda or Pergub, the Perda 2/2025 record carries only its Mengubah link to Perda 6/2023 and the Pergub 25/2025 record no amending link, and lovebali.baliprov.go.id still states IDR 150,000 under Perda 2/2025 | Bali Provincial Govt JDIH via lovebali.baliprov.go.id (gubernur/v5/8.pdf Perda 2-2025 + gubernur/v5/9.pdf Pergub 25-2025 + gubernur/v4/8.BaliGovernorRegulation.pdf Perda 6-2023) cross-confirmed at peraturan.go.id and peraturan.bpk.go.id + `https://lovebali.baliprov.go.id/gubernur/v5/9.pdf` + Bali JDIH register `https://dashboard-jdih.baliprov.go.id/api/v1/laws?term=pungutan` | 2026-10-21 | 2027-01-19 | 2 | --rental`
- `2026-02-24 | rental | Bali Perda 4/2026 tentang Pengendalian Alih Fungsi Lahan Produktif dan Larangan Alih Kepemilikan Lahan Secara Nomine — ditetapkan and diundangkan Denpasar 24 Feb 2026, in force on promulgation (Pasal 26), LD Provinsi Bali 2026 No. 4, signed Wayan Koster. WHAT IT PROHIBITS: Pasal 13(2) bars conversion of Lahan Produktif designated in the spatial plan (exceptions in 13(3) for national strategic policy, public-interest land acquisition and disaster relocation); Pasal 14 bars any person from acting as "perantara, fasilitator, atau menyediakan sarana yang menyebabkan warga negara asing menguasai kepemilikan lahan secara Nomine", Nomine being defined (Pasal 1 angka 11) as an arrangement naming someone to hold or manage land rights on behalf of a foreign national as beneficial owner. SANCTIONS ARE ADMINISTRATIVE: Pasal 20(2) lists written warning, temporary suspension, site closure, licence revocation or annulment, demolition, restoration of land function, incentive withdrawal and administrative fine; Pasal 20(5) adds only that criminal sanctions may apply "sesuai dengan ketentuan peraturan perundang-undangan". The Perda does not itself create a criminal offence or a prison term for nominee arrangements. THE 5-YEAR + IDR 1bn FIGURE is UU 41/2009 Pasal 72(1), for an individual converting designated LP2B land contrary to Pasal 44(1); UU 41/2009 appears in the Perda's Mengingat (legal basis), not in its sanctions article, and it is a land-conversion penalty, not a nominee penalty (corporate officers: Pasal 74, 2-7 yr + IDR 2-7bn). OTA NIB: the 31 Mar 2026 date was superseded — Kemenpar's own release says OTAs will require NIB + KBLI + NKU checked against OSS through an API targeted to launch June 2027; the Minister's 26 May 2026 press conference announced delisting of ~1,600 verified unlicensed operators from 1 Aug 2026 (secondary: ANTARA, Kontan, Media Indonesia); Kemenpar's own 26 May 2026 release gives no ~1,600 figure and no 1 Aug date; it says Kemenpar will send OTAs a list of unlicensed accommodation and the OTAs will delist within two months of that communication. Kemenpar's 20 Oct 2026 two-year release says it worked with nine OTAs on 'sosialisasi dan penerapan kebijakan termasuk delisting' and that short-stay accommodation units with an NIB reached 113,692 as at 23 Sep 2026, but it gives no count of listings delisted. Delisting outcome, secondary only: per Kemenpar spokesperson Imam Priyono via Kontan (9 Aug 2026), after a 31 Jul 2026 licensing deadline 259 of about 1,367 listed accommodations had been delisted across the nine OTA partners by 1 Aug 2026, 397 had obtained a licence, 195 were no longer found on the platforms and 515 are targeted in the next delisting stage; Kontan gives 1 Jun 2027 for the API start; no primary Kemenpar count found. The measure is national, not Bali-only. INVESTMENT LIST (corrected 2026-10-21): Perpres 10/2021 Lampiran II, as replaced by Perpres 49/2021 (ditetapkan 24 May 2021, diundangkan 25 May 2021, LN 2021 No. 128; Perpres 10/2021 status Berlaku and amended only by Perpres 49/2021 per JDIH Kemenko Infra), lists under item 48 Jasa Penginapan Hotel Bintang 1 (KBLI 55110), Hotel Melati (55120), Pondok Wisata (55130), Guest House (55199) and Vila (55193) as 'Dialokasikan untuk Koperasi dan UMKM', with the Kemitraan column blank. How OSS treats a PT PMA applying for KBLI 55193 was not verified — verify at BKPM (bkpm.go.id) and OSS (oss.go.id). Still NOT RE-VERIFIED from primary text: that Permenpar 18/2016 reserves Pondok Wisata to Indonesian citizens — data not publicly available in primary text read — verify at Kemenpar JDIH (jdih.kemenpar.go.id); the KBLI 55193 villa and 55130 pondok wisata standards themselves sit in Permenpar 6/2025, see that entry | JDIH Provinsi Bali — Perda 4/2026 signed salinan (dashboard-jdih.baliprov.go.id API record b0de5129-0933-454f-91b7-8de70934b5d7) + UU 41/2009 via peraturan.bpk.go.id/Download/28115 + Kemenpar siaran pers kemenpar.go.id/berita/siaran-pers-kemenpar-kembangkan-sistem-api-bersama-ota-perkuat-tata-kelola-akomodasi-pariwisata + (secondary) sumbar.antaranews.com/berita/763892 + (secondary) https://industri.kontan.co.id/news/kemenpar-259-akomodasi-tak-berizin-sudah-dihapus-dari-platform-ota-per-agustus-2026 + Perpres 49/2021 Lampiran II via OSS `https://s3.oss.go.id/oss/cms/Lampiran-II-Peraturan-Presiden-Nomor-49-Tahun-2021-9168208c211b097945b476f6a4024ea1.pdf` + Kemenpar siaran pers kemenpar.go.id/berita/siaran-pers-pariwisata-tumbuh-positif-dalam-dua-tahun-pemerintahan-prabowo-gibran + (secondary) Kontan 9 Aug 2026 `https://industri.kontan.co.id/news/kemenpar-259-akomodasi-tak-berizin-sudah-dihapus-dari-platform-ota-per-agustus-2026` | 2026-10-21 | 2027-01-19 | 1 | --rental`

- `2026-07-28 | ownership | HDB resale — 15-month wait-out REMOVED for private residential property owners: with immediate effect from 28 Jul 2026, private residential property owners (PPOs) and ex-PPOs who buy a non-subsidised HDB resale flat without an HDB housing loan no longer serve the 15-month wait-out introduced with the 30 Sep 2022 cooling measures; they still need an HDB Flat Eligibility (HFE) letter, and a PPO must dispose of all private residential property, in Singapore or overseas, within six months of completing the resale purchase. - `2024-08-01 | foreign-buyer | 2024 Land Law (31/2024/QH15) + 2023 Housing Law (27/2023/QH15) both took effect 1 Aug 2024 — NOT 1 Aug 2025 — after Law 43/2024/QH15 (adopted 29 Jun 2024; Official Gazette Nos 987+988 of 25 Aug 2024) replaced Art. 252(1) Land Law and Art. 197(1) Housing Law to bring commencement forward five months from the originally enacted 1 Jan 2025. Foreign-buyer core still in force at 2026-10-21 (NA Office consolidated text 79/VBHN-VPQH 2026): Housing Law Art. 19(1) caps foreign holdings at 30% of the units in any one apartment building, or 250 landed houses (villas plus row houses) across an area of ward-equivalent population; Art. 20(2)(c) gives a foreign individual ownership per the contract but no more than 50 years from the date the Certificate is issued, extendable ONCE only by up to a further 50 years on application, term stated on the Certificate — ward-equivalence criterion and extension procedure delegated to Decree 95/2024/ND-CP (24 Jul 2024, in force 1 Aug 2024; since amended inter alia by Decree 54/2026/NĐ-CP of 9 Feb 2026 — whether any amendment touches the foreign-ownership articles not checked). PENDING, NOT LAW: a comprehensive Housing Law (amended) bill drafted by the Ministry of Construction is on the 2nd session of the XVI National Assembly (opened 17 Oct 2026; phase 1 to 14 Nov, phase 2 20-21 Nov, closing scheduled 21 Nov 2026, reserve 23 Nov) for consideration and adoption together with the amended Land Law and Real Estate Business Law; the drafts reviewed by the NA Standing Committee on 1-2 Oct 2026 drop foreign ownership of landed houses and allow apartments only (draft Art. 21, implementing Party Resolution 21-NQ/TW of 28 Jul 2026), and the bill as tabled in plenary on 22 Oct 2026 (Government Tờ trình 924/TTr-CP of 16 Oct 2026; 13 chapters, 130 articles) still does — NA portal: 'bỏ quy định về sở hữu nhà ở riêng lẻ của tổ chức, cá nhân nước ngoài' (removes foreign organisations' and individuals' ownership of landed houses). The tabled draft leaves the foreign apartment ratio in draft Art. 21(1) to the Government instead of the statutory 30%, and the NA Law and Justice Committee's verification report (1244/BC-UBPLTP16, 21 Oct 2026) asks for the ratio to be written into the law (secondary reports). Plenary debate held 28 Oct 2026 (NA press release no. 11); no adoption vote located as at 2026-10-28. Transition for existing foreign landed-house owners and the effective date in the tabled text: not verified (a 17 Sep 2026 secondary report on an earlier draft said existing owners and signed contracts continue under the 2023 Law; the NA Standing Committee asked for the three laws to take effect on the same day). - `2026-01-01 | tax | Land-price tables under the 2024 Land Law — CORRECTED: the Law (in force 1 Aug 2024) kept the 2013-law provincial tables to 31 Dec 2025 (Art. 257(1)) and had the provincial People's Council (HĐND), on the provincial People's Committee's submission, decide the first new table applied from 1 Jan 2026 and then adjust it yearly from 1 Jan (Art. 159(3)). NA Resolution 254/2025/QH15 (adopted 11 Dec 2025, in force 1 Jan 2026) Art. 7(3) now has the HĐND decide, publish and apply the table from 1 Jan 2026 and amend it 'when necessary' — no fixed annual rebuild — and Art. 8(2) has the provincial People's Committee issue a land-price adjustment coefficient applied from 1 Jan each year (article wording via luatvietnam reproduction; adoption and in-force dates via the Government portal). The non-agricultural land use tax base does NOT track each table revision: Law 48/2010/QH12 Art. 6(3) as amended by Land Law Art. 249 sets the per-m² price at the land-price-table value for the use, stabilised over a 5-year cycle — when the next cycle starts and which table it adopts: data not publicly available — verify at the provincial tax office. PENDING, NOT LAW: the comprehensive Land Law (amended) bill is on the 2nd session of the XVI National Assembly (17 Oct-21 Nov 2026). As tabled on 22 Oct 2026 it has 13 chapters and 112 articles (NA portal; supersedes the 9-chapter / 115-article count carried here earlier). The Government's Tờ trình says the Law must be issued before 1 Mar 2027 because the Government's land decentralisation and unblocking decrees made under NA Resolutions 190/2025/QH15 and 206/2025/QH15 lapse after 28 Feb 2027. The draft 'kế thừa và hoàn thiện' (carries over and refines) the land-price-table and adjustment-coefficient rules of the 2024 Law and Resolution 254/2025/QH15, and drops the case-specific land price (giá đất cụ thể). The Economic and Finance Committee asks the Government to show the effective date is feasible and to align it with the Housing and Real Estate Business laws. Plenary debate is set for 29 Oct 2026. - `ongoing (IGOC 2026 in force 2026-01-01) | foreign-buyer | Morocco exchange control — CORRECTED: what secures repatriation is the convertibility regime (garantie de transfert) for investments financed in foreign currency, not an investor 'registration' with the Office des Changes, and skipping it does NOT lock rental income or sale proceeds in dirhams outright. IGOC 2026 (Office des Changes, in force 1 Jan 2026): foreign investments, including acquisition of immovables and construction works, financed in foreign currency (transfer from abroad, debit of a foreign-currency or convertible-dirham account; art. 173) get free transfer of income, of sale or liquidation proceeds and of proceeds from succession; the proof is the bank's settlement justificatif (e.g. Formule 2 currency purchase, Formule 4 convertible-dirham debit, avis de crédit), which the bank must issue and copy to the client — the foreign-investment chapter sets out no separate investor filing with the Office des Changes. Without foreign-currency financing: (a) rental and other investment income — banks may pay it to non-resident foreign persons without limit in amount or time whatever the financing mode; resident foreigners holding 10+ years without proof of FX financing may transfer the last closed year's income up to MAD 2,000,000 a year; (b) sale proceeds — net dirham proceeds (after taxes) go to a seller resident in Morocco, or into a compte convertible à terme for a non-resident foreign seller, transferable in four equal 25% tranches (first on credit, the rest on each anniversary; arts. 175 and 241); (c) sales between foreign nationals or to a Moroccan resident abroad may be settled directly abroad, the buyer inheriting the seller's convertibility status. For a sale-proceeds transfer the bank requires the original settlement justificatifs plus copies of the acte d'achat and acte de vente and proof of taxes paid | Office des Changes IGOC 2026 (foreign-investment chapter + investment-income section + comptes convertibles à terme) — https://www.oc.gov.ma/sites/default/files/reglementation/pdf/2026-01/IGOC%202026.pdf | 2026-10-21 | 2027-01-19 | 1 | --tax --currency`
